# I 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 2,884 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-i-000001"></a>
## I

**發音：** /aɪ/
*pron.* 1. *(first-person, personal, pronoun)* **我, 俺** — The speaker or writer, referred to as the grammatical subject, of a sentence.

<a id="entry-i-000002"></a>
## i

*n.* 1. **碘** — a nonmetallic element belonging to the halogens; used especially in medicine and photography and in dyes; occurs naturally only in combination in small quantities (as in sea water or rocks)
2. **數目1** — the smallest whole number or a numeral representing this number
*“he has the one but will need a two and three to go with it”*
*“they had lunch at one”*
3. the 9th letter of the Roman alphabet
*adj.* 1. **一** — used of a single unit or thing; not two or more
*“`ane' is Scottish”*

<a id="entry-i-000003"></a>
## I am a doctor

*phr.* 1. **我是醫生 /我是醫生** — I am a doctor (a physician, a member of the medical profession)

<a id="entry-i-000004"></a>
## I am afraid of this

*phr.* 1. **我害怕這個 /我害怕這個** — Indicates that the speaker is afraid of something.

<a id="entry-i-000005"></a>
## I am English

*phr.* 1. **我是英格蘭人 /我是英格蘭人** — Indicates that the speaker is from England.

<a id="entry-i-000006"></a>
## I am HIV positive

*phr.* 1. **我是一個艾滋病感染者 /我是一個艾滋病感染者** — Indicates that the speaker is a carrier of the human immunodeficiency virus.

<a id="entry-i-000007"></a>
## I came, I saw, I conquered

**發音：** /ˌaɪ̯ ˈkeɪ̯m | ˌaɪ̯ ˈsɔː | ˌaɪ̯ ˈkɒŋ.kə(ɹ)d/
*phr.* 1. **我來，我見，我勝 /我來, 我見, 我勝** — I have gained a total and swift victory.

<a id="entry-i-000008"></a>
## I can't find my ...

*phr.* 1. **我找不到我的……, 我的……丟了 /我的……丟了, 我的……不見了 /我的……不見了** — Said after losing something.

<a id="entry-i-000009"></a>
## I can't swim

*phr.* 1. **我不會游泳 /我不會游泳** — Indicates the speaker does not know how to swim.

<a id="entry-i-000010"></a>
## I Ching

**發音：** /ˌiː ˈt͡ʃɪŋ/
*prop. n.* 1. **易經 /易經, 周易** — A Chinese classic text describing an ancient system of cosmology and philosophy which is at the heart of traditional Chinese cultural beliefs.

<a id="entry-i-000011"></a>
## I could eat a horse

*phr.* 1. *(excessive, idiomatic)* **我餓死了 /我餓死了** — I am very hungry.

<a id="entry-i-000012"></a>
## I do

*phr.* 1. **我願意 /我願意** — A traditional phrase said by each of the prospective spouses during a wedding ceremony in order to accept the marriage.

<a id="entry-i-000013"></a>
## I don't care

*phr.* 1. **我不在乎** — Indicates that the speaker has no interest or emotional investment in the topic at hand.

<a id="entry-i-000014"></a>
## I don't drive

*phr.* 1. **我不會開車 /我不會開車** — I don't drive (a car, a vehicle)

<a id="entry-i-000015"></a>
## I don't eat beef

*phr.* 1. **我不吃牛肉** — Indicates that the speaker does not eat beef.

<a id="entry-i-000016"></a>
## I don't eat fish

*phr.* 1. **我不吃魚 /我不吃魚** — Indicates that the speaker does not eat fish.

<a id="entry-i-000017"></a>
## I don't eat meat

*phr.* 1. **我不吃肉** — Indicates that the speaker does not eat meat.

<a id="entry-i-000018"></a>
## I don't eat pork

*phr.* 1. **我不吃豬肉 /我不吃豬肉** — Indicates that the speaker does not eat pork.

<a id="entry-i-000019"></a>
## I don't have all day

*phr.* 1. **別浪費我的時間 /別浪費我的時間** — Used to say that you want someone to do something faster because you do not have enough time to wait for them to finish.

<a id="entry-i-000020"></a>
## I don't know

**發音：** [ɑ̈ɪ̯(ɾ)oʊ̯̃(ʔ)(t̚)noʊ̯]
*interj.* 1. **我不知道** — Used in response to a question or command in which the target person does not know the answer to or how to respond.

<a id="entry-i-000021"></a>
## I don't speak English

*phr.* 1. **我不會說英語 /我不會說英語, 我不會講英語 /我不會講英語, 我不會說中文 /我不會說中文, 我不會講中文 /我不會講中文 我不會說官話 /我不會說官話, 我不會講官話 /我不會講官話** — Indicates that the speaker is unable to speak the English language.

<a id="entry-i-000022"></a>
## I don't think so

**發音：** /ˌaɪ ˌdəʊnt ˈθɪŋk(ˌ)səʊ/
*phr.* 1. **我不這樣認為 /我不這樣認為, 我覺得不是 /我覺得不是, 我不覺得 /我不覺得, 我可不這麼想 /我可不這麼想, 好像不是** — I think what has been said is untrue.
2. *(sarcastic)* **別瞎說 /別瞎說, 才怪, 豈有此理 /豈有此理** — No, absolutely not (used to refuse or decline something proposed).

<a id="entry-i-000023"></a>
## I don't understand

*phr.* 1. **我不明白, 我不懂** — Indicates that the speaker is not understanding the current subject or situation.

<a id="entry-i-000024"></a>
## I don't want it

*phr.* 1. **我不要** — Indicates that the speaker does not want to take something that is being offered.

<a id="entry-i-000025"></a>
## I don't want to buy anything

*phr.* 1. **我什麼都不想買 /我什麼都不想買** — I don't want to buy anything

<a id="entry-i-000026"></a>
## I don't want to talk about it

*phr.* 1. **我不想再談這事了 /我不想再談這事了** — Indicates that the speaker does not want to discuss a particular topic.

<a id="entry-i-000027"></a>
## I hate you

*phr.* 1. **我恨你, 我討厭你 /我討厭你** — Expression of hatred, or intense disdain or dislike directed at someone.

<a id="entry-i-000028"></a>
## I have a cold

**發音：** /aɪ hæv ə koʊld/
*phr.* 1. **我感冒了** — I am suffering from a cold.

<a id="entry-i-000029"></a>
## I have a disability

*phr.* 1. *(no-gloss, translation-hub)* **我有殘疾 /我有殘疾**

<a id="entry-i-000030"></a>
## I have a fever

*phr.* 1. **我發燒了 /我發燒了** — Indicates that the speaker has an abnormally high body temperature, which is usually a symptom of a disease.

<a id="entry-i-000031"></a>
## I have a headache

*phr.* 1. **我頭疼 /我頭疼, 我頭痛 /我頭痛** — Indicates that the speaker's head hurts.

<a id="entry-i-000032"></a>
## I have a question

*phr.* 1. **我有一個問題 /我有一個問題** — Indicates that the speaker wants to ask a question.

<a id="entry-i-000033"></a>
## I have a toothache

*phr.* 1. **我牙疼** — Indicates that the speaker's tooth hurts.

<a id="entry-i-000034"></a>
## I have AIDS

*phr.* 1. **我有艾滋病** — Indicates that the speaker has acquired immune deficiency syndrome.

<a id="entry-i-000035"></a>
## I have asthma

*phr.* 1. **我有哮喘** — Indicates that the speaker is afflicted with asthma

<a id="entry-i-000036"></a>
## I have cancer

*phr.* 1. **我有癌症** — Indicates that the speaker is afflicted with some form of cancer.

<a id="entry-i-000037"></a>
## I have diabetes

*phr.* 1. **我有糖尿病** — Indicates that the speaker has diabetes.

<a id="entry-i-000038"></a>
## I have high blood pressure

*phr.* 1. **我有高血壓 /我有高血壓** — Indicates that the speaker has hypertension (high blood pressure).

<a id="entry-i-000039"></a>
## I have low blood pressure

*phr.* 1. **我有低血壓 /我有低血壓** — Indicates that the speaker has hypotension (low blood pressure).

<a id="entry-i-000040"></a>
## I have no money

*phr.* 1. **我沒有錢 /我沒有錢** — Indicates that the speaker has no money.

<a id="entry-i-000041"></a>
## I hope you're happy

*phr.* 1. *(sarcastic)* **你開心就好 /你開心就好** — Said to scold someone who did something wrong, after seeing the consequences for the speaker.

<a id="entry-i-000042"></a>
## I know

*phr.* 1. **我知道** — A response used to indicate that speaker was in agreement with the preceding statement before it was made.
2. **我知道了** — An emphatic assertion that one has a solution, an answer, or an idea.

<a id="entry-i-000043"></a>
## I like reading

*phr.* 1. **我喜歡讀書 /我喜歡讀書, 我喜歡閱讀 /我喜歡閱讀, 我很喜歡看書 /我很喜歡看書** — Indicates that the speaker likes reading books.

<a id="entry-i-000044"></a>
## I like you

*phr.* 1. **我喜歡你 /我喜歡你** — Indicates that the speaker likes the interlocutor.

<a id="entry-i-000045"></a>
## I live in Melbourne

*phr.* 1. **我住在墨爾本 /我住在墨爾本** — Explains that the person lives in Melbourne (example city).

<a id="entry-i-000046"></a>
## I lost my backpack

*phr.* 1. **我的揹包丟了 /我的揹包丟了** — Indicates that the speaker has lost his or her backpack.

<a id="entry-i-000047"></a>
## I lost my glasses

*phr.* 1. **我丟了我的眼鏡 /我丟了我的眼鏡** — Indicates that the speaker has lost his or her glasses.

<a id="entry-i-000048"></a>
## I lost my handbag

*phr.* 1. **我丟了我的手提包 /我丟了我的手提包** — Indicates that the speaker has lost his or her handbag.

<a id="entry-i-000049"></a>
## I lost my keys

*phr.* 1. *(US)* **我丟了鑰匙 /我丟了鑰匙** — Indicates that the speaker has lost his or her keys.

<a id="entry-i-000050"></a>
## I lost my wallet

*phr.* 1. **我丟了錢包 /我丟了錢包, 我的錢包不見了 /我的錢包不見了** — Indicates that the speaker has lost his or her wallet.

<a id="entry-i-000051"></a>
## I love you

**發音：** /aɪ ˈlʌv juː/
*phr.* 1. **我愛你 /我愛你** — An affirmation of affection or deep caring, especially to a family member.
2. **我愛你 /我愛你** — An affirmation of romantic feeling to a lover or spouse.
3. **我很喜歡你 /我很喜歡你** — A platonic expression of strong inclination or liking to a friend.

<a id="entry-i-000052"></a>
## I miss you

*phr.* 1. **我想你了** — An expression of sorrow or sadness about the absence of a family member, close friend, lover or spouse.

<a id="entry-i-000053"></a>
## I must go

*phr.* 1. **我必須走了 /我必須走了, 我應該走了 /我應該走了** — I must leave

<a id="entry-i-000054"></a>
## I need ...

*phr.* 1. *(no-gloss, translation-hub)* **我需要……**

<a id="entry-i-000055"></a>
## I need a doctor

*phr.* 1. **我需要一位醫生 /我需要一位醫生** — A request for a doctor

<a id="entry-i-000056"></a>
## I need a guide

*phr.* 1. **我需要一位導遊 /我需要一位導遊** — Indicates that the speaker needs a person to guide him or her.

<a id="entry-i-000057"></a>
## I need an interpreter

*phr.* 1. **我需要一位口譯員 /我需要一位口譯員** — Indicates that the speaker needs an interpreter.

<a id="entry-i-000058"></a>
## I need to vomit

**發音：** /aɪ nid tu ˈvɑmit/
*phr.* 1. *(no-gloss, translation-hub)* **我想吐**

<a id="entry-i-000059"></a>
## I rest my case

*phr.* 1. **我的觀點證明完畢 /我的觀點證明完畢** — My argument is proven; I conclude my statement.
2. *(idiomatic)* **上述概括了我的觀點 /上述概括了我的觀點** — The previous statement or proclamation encapsulates my view.

<a id="entry-i-000060"></a>
## I see

*interj.* 1. *(informal)* **懂了, 明白了, 原來如此 /原來如此** — I understand.

<a id="entry-i-000061"></a>
## I think so

*phr.* 1. **我想是這樣 /我想是這樣, 我這麼認為 /我這麼認為** — I believe that is the case, but am not certain.

<a id="entry-i-000062"></a>
## I think, therefore I am

*phr.* 1. **我思故我在** — I am able to think, therefore I exist; a proof of existence based on the fact that someone capable of any form of thought necessarily exists.

<a id="entry-i-000063"></a>
## I told you

*phr.* 1. *(informal, ironic)* **我跟你說過 /我跟你說過** — Used to inform the listener that a statement or affirmation made before was true, despite the listener's doubts.

<a id="entry-i-000064"></a>
## I told you so

**發音：** /aɪ̯ təʊ̯ld juː səʊ̯/
*phr.* 1. *(informal, ironic)* **我說什麼來著 /我說什麼來著, 我說什麼來著** — Used to remind someone that they were already warned by the speaker or writer that a certain event would happen.

<a id="entry-i-000065"></a>
## I want to go to the toilet

*phr.* 1. **我想上個廁所 /我想上個廁所** — Indicates that the speaker feels the urge to go the toilet.

<a id="entry-i-000066"></a>
## I want to know

*phr.* 1. **我想知道, 我要知道** — A request for information.

<a id="entry-i-000067"></a>
## I was born in ...

*phr.* 1. **我在……年出生** — I was born in ... (year).
2. **我在……出生** — I was born in ... (place name).

<a id="entry-i-000068"></a>
## I wish

*phr.* 1. *(colloquial, idiomatic)* **但願 /但願** — Used to express the speaker's wish that the preceding statement were true.

<a id="entry-i-000069"></a>
## I'd like to kiss you

*phr.* 1. **我想吻你** — Used to ask to kiss a person.

<a id="entry-i-000070"></a>
## I'd like to know

*phr.* 1. **我想知道, 我想了解一下** — A polite request for information.

<a id="entry-i-000071"></a>
## I'll call the police

*phr.* 1. **我會叫警察 /我會叫警察, 我會報警 /我會報警** — Indicates that the speaker is going to call the police, presumably to counter actions from the interlocutor.

<a id="entry-i-000072"></a>
## I'll leave it up to you

*phr.* 1. **你來決定吧 /你來決定吧** — I will let you be in charge; you can make the decisions or choices.

<a id="entry-i-000073"></a>
## I'll pass

*phr.* 1. **我不要了** — No thank you: used when asked whether one wants or needs something, etc.

<a id="entry-i-000074"></a>
## I'm

**發音：** /aɪm/
*phr.* 1. *(abbreviation, alt-of, contraction)* **我是** — Contraction of I + am.

<a id="entry-i-000075"></a>
## I'm ... year(s) old

*phr.* 1. **我……歲了 /我……歲了, 我……歲 /我……歲** — I was born ... year(s) ago.

*phr.* 1. **我信佛** — Indicates that the speaker is a Buddhist.

*phr.* 1. **我信天主教** — Indicates that the speaker is a Catholic.

## I'm a Christian

*phr.* 1. **我信基督教** — Indicates that the speaker is a Christian.

## I'm a girl

*phr.* 1. **我是女的** — Indicates that the speaker is female.

<a id="entry-i-000080"></a>
## I'm a guy

*phr.* 1. *(informal)* **我是男的** — Indicates that the speaker is male.

<a id="entry-i-000081"></a>
## I'm a Muslim

*phr.* 1. **我是穆斯林** — Indicates that the speaker is a Muslim.

*phr.* 1. **我是新教徒** — Indicates that the speaker is a Protestant.

<a id="entry-i-000083"></a>
## I'm a vegan

*phr.* 1. **我是嚴格素食主義者 /我是嚴格素食主義者** — Indicates that the speaker is a vegan.

<a id="entry-i-000084"></a>
## I'm a vegetarian

*phr.* 1. **我吃素** — Indicates that the speaker is a vegetarian.

<a id="entry-i-000085"></a>
## I'm afraid not

*phr.* 1. **恐怕不是, 恐怕沒有 /恐怕沒有, 恐怕不行** — Unfortunately, no; I regret that that is not so.

<a id="entry-i-000086"></a>
## I'm afraid so

*phr.* 1. **恐怕是的, 恐怕有** — Unfortunately, yes; I regret that that is so.

<a id="entry-i-000087"></a>
## I'm agender

*phr.* 1. **我是無性別者 /我是無性別者** — Indicates that the speaker is agender.

## I'm agnostic

*phr.* 1. **我是不可知論者 /我是不可知論者** — Indicates that the speaker is agnostic.

## I'm allergic to aspirin

*phr.* 1. **我對阿斯匹靈過敏 /我對阿斯匹靈過敏** — Indicates that the speaker has an allergy to aspirin.

## I'm allergic to nuts

*phr.* 1. **我對堅果過敏 /我對堅果過敏** — Indicates that the speaker has an allergy to nuts.

## I'm allergic to penicillin

*phr.* 1. **我對青黴素過敏 /我對青黴素過敏** — Indicates that the speaker has an allergy to penicillin.

## I'm an atheist

*phr.* 1. **我是無神論者 /我是無神論者** — Indicates that the speaker is an atheist.

## I'm aromantic

*phr.* 1. **我是無浪漫傾曏者 /我是無浪漫傾向者** — Indicates that the speaker is aromantic.

## I'm asexual

*phr.* 1. **我是無性戀 /我是無性戀** — Indicates that the speaker is asexual.

## I'm ashamed

*phr.* 1. **我感到很羞愧** — Indicates that the speaker is ashamed.

<a id="entry-i-000096"></a>
## I'm bisexual

*phr.* 1. **我是雙性戀 /我是雙性戀** — Indicates that the speaker is bisexual.

<a id="entry-i-000097"></a>
## I'm bleeding

*phr.* 1. **我出血了** — Indicates that the speaker is bleeding.

<a id="entry-i-000098"></a>
## I'm blind

**發音：** /aɪm ˈblaɪnd/
*phr.* 1. **我眼瞎了, 我失明瞭, 我是盲人, 我目盲** — Indicates that the speaker is blind.

<a id="entry-i-000099"></a>
## I'm busy

*phr.* 1. **我很忙, 我有事情** — I'm busy.

<a id="entry-i-000100"></a>
## I'm cold

*phr.* 1. **我很冷** — Indicates that the speaker feels cold.

<a id="entry-i-000101"></a>
## I'm deaf

**發音：** /aɪm ˈdɛf/
*phr.* 1. **我聾了 /我聾了** — Indicates that one is deaf.

<a id="entry-i-000102"></a>
## I'm divorced

*phr.* 1. **我離婚了 /我離婚了** — Indicates that the speaker is divorced.

## I'm dying

*phr.* 1. **我快死了** — Indicates that the speaker believes that they are dying.

<a id="entry-i-000104"></a>
## I'm embarrassed

*phr.* 1. **我感到很尷尬 /我感到很尷尬, 我很不好意思** — Indicates that the speaker is embarrassed.

<a id="entry-i-000105"></a>
## I'm fine

*phr.* 1. **我很好** — Conventional response to "how are you?", indicating that one is well.

<a id="entry-i-000106"></a>
## I'm fine, thank you

*phr.* 1. **我很好，謝謝 /我很好, 謝謝** — The expected, polite response to how are you?

<a id="entry-i-000107"></a>
## I'm full

*phr.* 1. **我吃飽了 /我吃飽了** — Indicates that the speaker does not wish to continue eating.

<a id="entry-i-000108"></a>
## I'm gay

*phr.* 1. **我是同性戀 /我是同性戀, 我是同志** — Indicates that the speaker is gay.

<a id="entry-i-000109"></a>
## I'm hot

*phr.* 1. **我很熱 /我很熱** — Indicates that the speaker is hot (i.e. feeling the sensation of heat, especially to the point of discomfort)

<a id="entry-i-000110"></a>
## I'm hungry

*phr.* 1. **我餓了 /我餓了, 我很餓 /我很餓** — I have a strong appetite to eat.

<a id="entry-i-000111"></a>
## I'm in love with you

*phr.* 1. **我愛上你了 /我愛上你了** — A declaration of passion or romantic feeling.

<a id="entry-i-000112"></a>
## I'm Jewish

*phr.* 1. **我是猶太人 /我是猶太人, 我信猶太教 /我信猶太教** — Indicates that the speaker is of Jewish ethnic descent.

<a id="entry-i-000113"></a>
## I'm looking for a grocery store

*phr.* 1. **我在找一家雜貨店 /我在找一家雜貨店** — Indicates that the speaker is looking for a grocery store.

<a id="entry-i-000114"></a>
## I'm looking for a job

*phr.* 1. **我在找工作, 我要求職 /我要求職** — Indicates that the speaker is looking for a job.

## I'm lost

*phr.* 1. **我迷路了** — Indicates that the speaker is unable to find his or her way.

<a id="entry-i-000116"></a>
## I'm married

*phr.* 1. **我結婚了 /我結婚了** — Indicates that the speaker has a spouse.

<a id="entry-i-000117"></a>
## I'm mute

**發音：** /aɪm ˈmjuːt/
*phr.* 1. **我是個啞巴 /我是個啞巴** — I'm mute

<a id="entry-i-000118"></a>
## I'm not interested

*phr.* 1. *(no-gloss, translation-hub)* **我沒興趣 /我沒興趣**

<a id="entry-i-000119"></a>
## I'm not religious

*phr.* 1. **我不信教** — Indicates that the speaker is a not a follower of any religious doctrines.

<a id="entry-i-000170"></a>
## ice

**發音：** /aɪs/
*n.* 1. **冰, 冰塊** — water frozen in the solid state
*“Americans like ice in their drinks”*
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **冰** — Water in frozen (solid) form.
3. **寶石, 鑽石** — diamonds
*“look at the ice on that dame!”*
4. **梅太德林, 甲基苯丙胺, 脫氧麻黃鹼, 中樞興奮藥** — an amphetamine derivative (trade name Methedrine) used in the form of a crystalline hydrochloride; used as a stimulant to the nervous system and as an appetite suppressant
5. **內燃機** — a heat engine in which combustion occurs inside the engine rather than in a separate furnace; heat expands a gas that either moves a piston or turns a gas turbine
6. *(countable, uncountable, usually)* **冰沙, 思慕雪, 刨冰, 雪泥, 雪芭** — A frozen dessert made of fruit juice, water and sugar.
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **冰凍** — To become ice; to freeze.
2. **冰凍** — put ice on or put on ice
*“Ice your sprained limbs”*
3. decorate with frosting
*“frost a cake”*
4. cause to become ice or icy
*“an iced summer drink”*

<a id="entry-i-000171"></a>
## ice age

**發音：** /ˈaɪ.seɪd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. **冰川期, 冰期, 冰河時代, 冰河時期, 冰河期** — any period of time during which glaciers covered a large part of the earth's surface
*“the most recent ice age was during the Pleistocene”*
2. *(colloquial)* **冰河時期 /冰河時期, 冰期, 冰河期** — Any of several glacial periods (within a scientific ice age) marked by episodes of extensive glaciation alternating with episodes of relative warmth (interglacial).

## idea

**發音：** /aɪ̯ˈdɪə̯/
*n.* 1. **主意, 思維, 構想, 理念, 思想** — the content of cognition; the main thing you are thinking about
*“it was not a good idea”*
*“the thought never entered my mind”*
2. **近似值, 估計, 判斷, 估價, 評價** — an approximate calculation of quantity or degree or worth
*“an estimate of what it would cost”*
*“a rough idea how long it would take”*
3. **構想 /構想, 構思 /構思, 理念** — An abstract archetype of a given thing, compared to which real-life examples are seen as imperfect approximations; pure essence, as opposed to actual examples.
4. **主意, 思想, 想法** — An image of an object that is formed in the mind or recalled by the memory.
5. **主題** — (music) melodic subject of a musical composition
*“the theme is announced in the first measures”*
*“the accompanist picked up the idea and elaborated it”*
6. **構想 /構想** — A conception in the mind of something to be done; a plan for doing something, an intention.

<a id="entry-i-000285"></a>
## ideal

**發音：** /aɪˈdɪəl/
*adj.* 1. **理想的** — Optimal; being the best possibility.
2. **唯心論+的** — of or relating to the philosophical doctrine of the reality of ideas
3. conforming to an ultimate standard of perfection or excellence; embodying an ideal
4. constituting or existing only in the form of an idea or mental image or conception
*“a poem or essay may be typical of its period in idea or ideal content”*
*n.* 1. **無以匹敵的人, 無雙的人** — model of excellence or perfection of a kind; one having no equal
2. **典範, 理想** — A perfect standard of beauty, intellect etc., or a standard of excellence to aim at.
3. **理想** — A two-sided ideal; a subset of a ring which is closed under both left and right multiplication by elements of the ring.
4. **理想** — A non-empty lower set (of a partially ordered set) which is closed under binary suprema (a.k.a. joins).
5. the idea of something that is perfect; something that one hopes to attain

<a id="entry-i-000286"></a>

<a id="entry-i-000287"></a>

<a id="entry-i-000288"></a>

<a id="entry-i-000289"></a>

2. *(countable, uncountable)* **唯心主義 /唯心主義** — An approach to philosophical enquiry, which asserts that direct and immediate knowledge can only be had of ideas or mental pictures.
3. (philosophy) the philosophical theory that ideas are the only reality
4. impracticality by virtue of thinking of things in their ideal form rather than as they really are
5. elevated ideals or conduct; the quality of believing that ideals should be pursued

<a id="entry-i-000290"></a>

2. **全冪等+的, 冪等+的, 等冪+的** — unchanged in value following multiplication by itself
*“this matrix is idempotent”*

## identical

**發音：** /ɪˈdɛntɪkl̩/
*adj.* 1. **不能區別+的, 完全相同+的, 相同+的, 難以分辨+的, 同一+的** — exactly alike; incapable of being perceived as different
*“rows of identical houses”*
*“cars identical except for their license plates”*
2. **一模一樣+的, 同一+的, 完全相同+的, 同樣+的** — being the exact same one; not any other:
*“this is the identical room we stayed in before”*
*“the themes of his stories are one and the same”*
3. *(not-comparable)* **相同, 同一** — Bearing full likeness by having precisely the same set of characteristics; indistinguishable.
4. *(not-comparable)* **同卵** — Of twins, sharing the same genetic code.
5. (of twins) derived from a single egg or ovum
*“identical twins are monovular”*
6. having properties with uniform values along all axes

<a id="entry-i-000299"></a>
## identically

*adv.* 1. **一致+地, 同一+地, 同樣+地** — with complete identity; in an identical manner
*“he is fitted with an identically similar one”*

<a id="entry-i-000300"></a>
## identicalness

*n.* 1. **同一, 恆等, 相同** — exact sameness
*“they shared an identity of interests”*

<a id="entry-i-000301"></a>
## identifiably

*adv.* 1. **可以確認+地, 可認明+地, 可辨認+地** — in an identifiable manner
*“they were identifiably different”*

<a id="entry-i-000302"></a>
## identification

**發音：** /aɪˌdɛntɪfɪˈkeɪʃn̩/
*n.* 1. **身份證明** — evidence of identity; something that identifies a person or thing
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **辨識 /辨識, 鑑定 /鑑定, 識別 /識別** — The act of identifying (i.e., which one, which thing).
3. **確認, 認出, 證明, 識別, 辨認** — the condition of having the identity (of a person or object) established
*“the thief's identification was followed quickly by his arrest”*
*“identification of the gun was an important clue”*
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **身份證 /身份證** — A document or documents serving as evidence of a person's identity.
5. the act of designating or identifying something
6. the process of recognizing something or someone by remembering
*“a politician whose recall of names was as remarkable as his recognition of faces”*
*“experimental psychologists measure the elapsed time from the onset of the stimulus to its recognition by the observer”*

<a id="entry-i-000303"></a>
## identifier

*n.* 1. **識別碼 /識別碼, 標誌符 /標誌符, 標識符** — A formal name used in source code to refer to a variable, function, procedure, package, etc. or in an operating system to refer to a process, user, group, etc.
2. a symbol that establishes the identity of the one bearing it

<a id="entry-i-000304"></a>
## identify

**發音：** /aɪˈdɛn.tɪ.faɪ/
*v.* 1. **列舉, 命名** — give the name or identifying characteristics of; refer to by name or some other identifying characteristic property
*“Many senators were named in connection with the scandal”*
*“The almanac identifies the auspicious months”*
2. *(transitive)* **辨認 /辨認, 識別 /識別** — To establish the identity of someone or something.
3. recognize as being; establish the identity of someone or something
*“She identified the man on the 'wanted' poster”*
4. consider (oneself) as similar to somebody else
*“He identified with the refugees”*
5. conceive of as united or associated
*“Sex activity is closely identified with the hypothalamus”*
6. identify as in botany or biology, for example

<a id="entry-i-000305"></a>
## identikit

**發音：** /aɪˈdɛntɪkɪt/
*n.* 1. **容貌拼圖 /容貌拼圖, 拼圖認人 /拼圖認人** — A picture of a person, reconstructed from strips showing facial features selected to match witnesses' descriptions; used by the police to construct a likeness of a person sought for a crime.
2. **合成人像, 容貌拼具, 容貌拼圖** — a likeness of a person's face constructed from descriptions given to police; uses a set of transparencies of various facial features that can be combined to build up a picture of the person sought

<a id="entry-i-000306"></a>
## identikit picture

*n.* 1. **合成人像, 容貌拼具, 容貌拼圖** — a likeness of a person's face constructed from descriptions given to police; uses a set of transparencies of various facial features that can be combined to build up a picture of the person sought

<a id="entry-i-000307"></a>
## identity

**發音：** /aɪˈdɛntɪti/
*n.* 1. **個性, 特性, 身份** — the distinct personality of an individual regarded as a persisting entity
*“you can lose your identity when you join the army”*
2. **恆等式** — an operator that leaves unchanged the element on which it operates
*“the identity under numerical multiplication is 1”*
3. **同一, 恆等, 相同** — exact sameness
*“they shared an identity of interests”*
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **個性 /個性, 身分, 身份** — Sameness, identicalness; the quality or fact of (several specified things) being the same.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **同一性, 個性 /個性, 特性, 身分, 身份** — The difference or character that marks off an individual or collective from the rest of the same kind; selfhood; the sense of who something or someone or oneself is, or the recurring characteristics that enable the recognition of such an individual or group by others or themselves.
6. *(countable, uncountable)* **身分, 身份** — A name or persona—a mask or appearance one presents to the world—by which one is known.

<a id="entry-i-000308"></a>
## identity card

*n.* 1. **身份證 /身份證, 證件 /證件** — A card or badge showing the official identity of the bearer.
2. **身份卡** — a card certifying the identity of the bearer
*“he had to show his card to get in”*

<a id="entry-i-000309"></a>
## identity crisis

*n.* 1. **個性危機** — distress and disorientation (especially in adolescence) resulting from conflicting pressures and uncertainty about one's self and one's role in society

<a id="entry-i-000310"></a>
## identity document

*n.* 1. **證件 /證件** — A document which may be used to prove a person's identity.

<a id="entry-i-000311"></a>
## identity element

*n.* 1. **恆等式** — an operator that leaves unchanged the element on which it operates
*“the identity under numerical multiplication is 1”*

<a id="entry-i-000312"></a>
## identity function

*n.* 1. **恆等式 /恆等式** — A function whose value is always the same as its independent variable, and for which the codomain equals the domain.

<a id="entry-i-000313"></a>
## identity matrix

*n.* 1. **單位矩陣 /單位矩陣, 單位方陣 /單位方陣** — A diagonal matrix all of whose diagonal elements are equal to 1.
2. a scalar matrix in which all of the diagonal elements are unity

<a id="entry-i-000314"></a>
## identity operator

*n.* 1. **恆等式** — an operator that leaves unchanged the element on which it operates
*“the identity under numerical multiplication is 1”*

<a id="entry-i-000315"></a>
## identity politician

*n.* 1. **身份政治擁護者 /身份政治擁護者** — An advocate of or participant in identity politics.

<a id="entry-i-000316"></a>
## identity politics

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **身份政治** — Politics focusing on the self-interest and perspectives of people in various groupings, such as ethnicity, race, religion, gender, sexual orientation, and gender identity.

<a id="entry-i-000317"></a>
## identity theft

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **身份盜竊 /身份盜竊** — The deliberate assumption of another person's identity, usually to gain access to that person's finances or to frame a person for a crime.
2. the co-option of another person's personal information (e.g., name, Social Security number, credit card number, passport) without that person's knowledge and the fraudulent use of such knowledge

*“idle drifters”*
6. not in active use

*phr.* 1. **東西沒壞就別修它 /東西沒壞就別修它** — It is wise to leave something alone and to avoid attempting to correct, fix, or improve what is already sufficient (often with an implication that is the attempted improvement is risky and might backfire).

<a id="entry-i-000375"></a>
## if looks could kill

*phr.* 1. **如果眼神可以殺人** — Used to characterize a look of strong hostility.

<a id="entry-i-000376"></a>
## if only

*conj.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **但願 /但願, ...就好了** — I wish that; would (that) (archaic). Signifies a wish or desire for the present or the future.

<a id="entry-i-000377"></a>
## if the mountain won't come to Muhammad

*phr.* 1. **山不過來，我就過去 /山不過來, 我就過去** — If something one wishes to be done cannot be commanded to be done, one must find another way to achieve one's goal.

<a id="entry-i-000378"></a>
## if you can't beat them, join them

*phr.* 1. **如果打不過他們，就加入他們 /如果打不過他們, 就加入他們** — If one's adversaries are stronger, it is better to join their side.

<a id="entry-i-000379"></a>
## if you can't stand the heat, get out of the kitchen

<a id="entry-i-000381"></a>

## igneous

**發音：** /ˈɪɡ.ni.əs/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **火成的** — Pertaining to or having the nature of fire; containing fire; resembling fire.
2. **火成+的, 高熱所產生+的** — produced under conditions involving intense heat
*“igneous rock is rock formed by solidification from a molten state; especially from molten magma"”*
*“igneous fusion is fusion by heat alone”*
3. *(not-comparable)* **火成的** — Resulting from, or produced by, great heat. With rocks, it could also mean formed from lava or magma.
4. produced by the action of fire or intense heat
*“rocks formed by igneous agents”*
5. like or suggestive of fire
*“a fiery desert wind”*
*“an igneous desert atmosphere”*

<a id="entry-i-000397"></a>
## igneous rock

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **巖漿巖 /岩漿岩, 火成岩** — One of the major groups of rock that makes up the crust of the Earth; formed by the cooling of molten rock, either below the surface (intrusive) or on the surface (extrusive).
2. rock formed by the solidification of molten magma

<a id="entry-i-000398"></a>
## ignite

**發音：** /ɪɡˈnaɪt/
*v.* 1. **使灼熱, 點起, 使燃燒, 點火, 點燃** — cause to start burning; subject to fire or great heat
*“Great heat can ignite almost any dry matter”*
*“Light a cigarette”*
2. **開始燃燒, 燒起來, 著火, 點燃, 燃燒** — start to burn or burst into flames
*“Marsh gases ignited suddenly”*
*“The oily rags combusted spontaneously”*
3. *(transitive)* **引燃, 激發 /激發** — To spark off (something), to trigger
4. *(intransitive)* **點火 /點火, 著火** — To commence burning.
5. arouse or excite feelings and passions
*“The ostentatious way of living of the rich ignites the hatred of the poor”*
*“The refugees' fate stirred up compassion around the world”*

<a id="entry-i-000399"></a>
## igniter

<a id="entry-i-000445"></a>
## ill

**發音：** /ɪl/
*adj.* 1. **病+的, 有病+的, 不健康+的** — affected by an impairment of normal physical or mental function
*“ill from the monotony of his suffering”*
2. *(obsolete)* **病** — Evil; wicked (of people).
3. resulting in suffering or adversity
*“ill effects”*
*“it's an ill wind that blows no good”*
4. distressing
*“ill manners”*
*“of ill repute”*
5. indicating hostility or enmity
*“you certainly did me an ill turn”*
*“ill feelings”*
6. presaging ill fortune
*“ill omens”*
*“ill predictions”*
*adv.* 1. (`ill' is often used as a combining form) in a poor or improper or unsatisfactory manner; not well
*“he was ill prepared”*
*“it ill befits a man to betray old friends”*
2. unfavorably or with disapproval
*“tried not to speak ill of the dead”*
*“thought badly of him for his lack of concern”*
3. with difficulty or inconvenience; scarcely or hardly
*“we can ill afford to buy a new car just now”*
*n.* 1. **小病, 微恙, 病痛, 疾病** — an often persistent bodily disorder or disease; a cause for complaining

## illation

**發音：** /ɪˈleɪʃ(ə)n/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **演繹 /演繹, 推論 /推論, 推定** — The act of inferring or concluding, especially from a set of premises; a conclusion, a deduction.
2. the reasoning involved in drawing a conclusion or making a logical judgment on the basis of circumstantial evidence and prior conclusions rather than on the basis of direct observation

<a id="entry-i-000465"></a>
## illative

**發音：** /ɪˈleɪtɪv/
*n.* 1. **入格** — The illative case, or a word in that case.
*adj.* 1. **推理+的, 推理上+的, 推論+的, 推論上+的, 演繹+的** — relating to or having the nature of illation or inference
*“the illative faculty of the mind”*
2. resembling or dependent on or arrived at by inference
*“an illative conclusion”*
*“inferential reasoning”*
3. expressing or preceding an inference
*“`therefore' is an illative word”*

<a id="entry-i-000466"></a>
## illative case

*n.* 1. **入格** — A case used to indicate movement into something; for example, into the house.

<a id="entry-i-000467"></a>
## illegal

*“an illegal chess move”*
2. **非法的, 不合法的, 不法的** — Contrary to, forbidden, or not authorized by law, especially criminal law.

## illusion

*“they have the illusion that I am very wealthy”*

4. *(uncountable)* **幻覺 /幻覺, 幻想** — The state of being deceived or misled.
5. **假象, 奇術, 戲法, 把戲, 騙術** — an illusory feat; considered magical by naive observers
6. the act of deluding; deception by creating illusory ideas

*“Secret activities offer presidents the alluring but often illusory promise that they can achieve foreign policy goals without the bothersome debate and open decision that are staples of democracy”*

## image

**發音：** /ˈɪm.ɪdʒ/
*n.* 1. **形象** — an iconic mental representation
*“her imagination forced images upon her too awful to contemplate”*
2. **印象, 形象, 形像** — A characteristic of a person, group or company etc., style, manner of dress, how one is or wishes to be perceived by others.
3. **意象, 圖景 /圖景, 圖像 /圖像** — A mental picture of something not real or not present.
4. **圖片 /圖片, 鏡像 /鏡像, 映像** — A file that contains all information needed to produce a live working copy. (See disk image and image copy.)
5. **相似者, 翻版, 面貌酷似的人** — someone who closely resembles a famous person (especially an actor)
*“he could be Gingrich's double”*
*“she's the very image of her mother”*
6. **塑像, 畫像, 肖像** — a representation of a person (especially in the form of sculpture)
*“the coin bears an effigy of Lincoln”*
*“the emperor's tomb had his image carved in stone”*
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **反映** — To reflect, mirror.
2. *(transitive)* **成像** — To create an image of.
3. render visible, as by means of MRI
4. imagine; conceive of; see in one's mind
*“I can't see him on horseback!”*
*“I can see what will happen”*

<a id="entry-i-000527"></a>
## imagination

**發音：** /ɪˌmæd͡ʒəˈneɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **想象力** — the ability to form mental images of things or events
*“he could still hear her in his imagination”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **想像力** — The image-making power of the mind; the act of mentally creating or reproducing an object not previously perceived; the ability to create such images.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **想像** — Particularly, construction of false images; fantasizing.
4. the formation of a mental image of something that is not perceived as real and is not present to the senses
*“popular imagination created a world of demons”*
*“imagination reveals what the world could be”*
5. the ability to deal resourcefully with unusual problems
*“a man of resource”*

## immune

*n.* 1. a person who is immune to a particular infection
*adj.* 1. **有免疫力+的, 免疫+的** — relating to the condition of immunity
*“the immune system”*
2. **豁免+的, 免除+的** — secure against
*“immune from taxation as long as he resided in Bermuda”*

3. **免疫+的** — relating to or conferring immunity (to disease or infection)
4. (usually followed by `to') not affected by a given influence

## immune complex

<a id="entry-i-000598"></a>

<a id="entry-i-000599"></a>

<a id="entry-i-000600"></a>
## immune globulin

*n.* 1. **免疫球蛋白** — a class of proteins produced in lymph tissue in vertebrates and that function as antibodies in the immune response

<a id="entry-i-000601"></a>
## immune serum globulin

*n.* 1. **免疫球蛋白** — a class of proteins produced in lymph tissue in vertebrates and that function as antibodies in the immune response

<a id="entry-i-000602"></a>
## immune suppressant drug

*n.* 1. **免疫抑制劑** — a drug that lowers the body's normal immune response

<a id="entry-i-000603"></a>
## immune system

*n.* 1. **免疫系統 /免疫系統** — The system that differentiates self from non-self and protects the body from foreign substances and pathogenic organisms by producing an immune response. It includes organs such as the thymus, the spleen and lymph nodes; tissue such as bone marrow, and lymphoid tissues such as the tonsils; cells such as lymphocytes including the B cells and T cells, and cell products such as antibodies.
2. a system (including the thymus and bone marrow and lymphoid tissues) that protects the body from foreign substances and pathogenic organisms by producing the immune response

<a id="entry-i-000604"></a>
## immunised

*adj.* 1. **免疫了+的, 接種了+的** — having been rendered unsusceptible to a disease

<a id="entry-i-000605"></a>
## immunity

**發音：** /ɪˈmjuːnəti/
*n.* 1. **免疫, 豁免, 免除** — the state of not being susceptible
*“unsusceptibility to rust”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **免疫, 免疫力** — Protective resistance against disease.
3. **免疫** — (medicine) the condition in which an organism can resist disease
4. **免除, 豁免** — an act exempting someone
*“he was granted immunity from prosecution”*
5. the quality of being unaffected by something
*“immunity to criticism”*

<a id="entry-i-000606"></a>
## immunize

**發音：** /ˈɪm.ju.naɪz/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **使免疫** — To make someone or something immune to something.
2. *(transitive)* **使免疫** — To inoculate someone, and thus produce immunity from a disease.
3. law: grant immunity from prosecution
4. perform vaccinations or produce immunity in by inoculation
*“We vaccinate against scarlet fever”*
*“The nurse vaccinated the children in the school”*

<a id="entry-i-000607"></a>
## immunized

*adj.* 1. **免疫了+的, 接種了+的** — having been rendered unsusceptible to a disease

<a id="entry-i-000608"></a>
## immunizing agent

*n.* 1. **免疫劑, 免疫原** — any substance or organism that provokes an immune response (produces immunity) when introduced into the body

<a id="entry-i-000609"></a>
## immunochemical

*adj.* 1. **免疫化學+的** — of or relating to immunochemistry

<a id="entry-i-000610"></a>
## immunocompromised

*adj.* 1. **免疫功能低下的** — Having an immune system that has been impaired by disease or treatment.
2. unable to develop a normal immune response usually because of malnutrition or immunodeficiency or immunosuppressive therapy

<a id="entry-i-000611"></a>
## immunodeficiency

**發音：** /ɪˌmjuːnəʊdɪˈfɪʃənsi/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **免疫缺陷** — A depletion in the body's natural immune system, or in some component of it.
2. **免疫缺陷** — immunological disorder in which some part of the body's immune system is inadequate and resistance to infectious diseases is reduced

<a id="entry-i-000612"></a>
## immunoelectrophoresis

*n.* 1. **免疫電泳** — electrophoresis to separate antigens and antibodies

<a id="entry-i-000613"></a>
## immunogen

*n.* 1. **免疫劑, 免疫原** — any substance or organism that provokes an immune response (produces immunity) when introduced into the body

<a id="entry-i-000614"></a>
## immunoglobulin

*n.* 1. **免疫球蛋白** — Any of the glycoproteins in blood serum that respond to invasion by foreign antigens and that protect the host by removing pathogens; an antibody.
2. **免疫球蛋白** — a class of proteins produced in lymph tissue in vertebrates and that function as antibodies in the immune response

<a id="entry-i-000615"></a>
## immunoglobulin a

*n.* 1. **免疫球蛋白A** — one of the most common of the five major classes of immunoglobulins; the chief antibody in the membranes of the gastrointestinal and respiratory tracts

<a id="entry-i-000616"></a>
## immunoglobulin d

*n.* 1. **免疫球蛋白D** — one of the five major classes of immunoglobulins; present in blood serum in small amounts

<a id="entry-i-000617"></a>
## immunoglobulin E

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **免疫球蛋白E** — A class of antibody (or immunoglobulin "isotype") found in mammals.

<a id="entry-i-000618"></a>
## immunoglobulin e

*n.* 1. **免疫球蛋白E** — one of the five major classes of immunoglobulins; present primarily in the skin and mucous membranes

<a id="entry-i-000619"></a>
## immunoglobulin g

*n.* 1. **免疫球蛋白G** — one of the five major classes of immunoglobulins; the main antibody defense against bacteria

<a id="entry-i-000620"></a>
## immunoglobulin m

*n.* 1. **免疫球蛋白M** — one of the five major classes of immunoglobulins; involved in fighting blood infections and in triggering production of immunoglobulin G

<a id="entry-i-000621"></a>
## immunohistochemistry

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **免疫組織化學 /免疫組織化學** — The analytical process of finding proteins in cells of a tissue microtome section exploiting the principle of antibodies binding specifically to antigens in biological tissues.
2. **免疫組織化學** — an assay that shows specific antigens in tissues by the use of markers that are either fluorescent dyes or enzymes (such as horseradish peroxidase)

<a id="entry-i-000622"></a>
## immunologic

*adj.* 1. **免疫學+的** — of or relating to immunology

<a id="entry-i-000623"></a>
## immunological

*adj.* 1. **免疫學+的** — of or relating to immunology

<a id="entry-i-000624"></a>
## immunological disorder

*n.* 1. **免疫系統混亂** — a disorder of the immune system

<a id="entry-i-000625"></a>
## immunology

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **免疫學 /免疫學, 免疫科** — The branch of medicine that concerns the body's immune system.
2. the branch of medical science that studies the body's immune system

<a id="entry-i-000626"></a>
## immunosuppressant

*n.* 1. **免疫抑制劑 /免疫抑制劑** — An immunosuppressive agent.
2. **免疫抑制劑** — a drug that lowers the body's normal immune response

<a id="entry-i-000627"></a>
## immunosuppressed

*adj.* 1. **抑制免疫+的** — of persons whose immune response is inadequate

<a id="entry-i-000628"></a>
## immunosuppression

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **免疫抑制** — The suppression of the immune response, especially its active medical suppression by human agency (via immunosuppressant drugs) to treat autoimmune diseases or to prevent allograft rejection after transplant.
2. **免疫抑制** — lowering the body's normal immune response to invasion by foreign substances; can be deliberate (as in lowering the immune response to prevent rejection of a transplanted organ) or incidental (as a side effect of radiotherapy or chemotherapy for cancer)

<a id="entry-i-000629"></a>
## immunosuppressive

*n.* 1. **免疫抑制劑** — a drug that lowers the body's normal immune response
*adj.* 1. **免疫抑制+的** — of or relating to a substance that lowers the body's normal immune response and induces immunosuppression

<a id="entry-i-000630"></a>
## immunosuppressive drug

*n.* 1. **免疫抑制劑** — a drug that lowers the body's normal immune response

<a id="entry-i-000631"></a>
## immunosuppressor

*n.* 1. **免疫抑制劑** — a drug that lowers the body's normal immune response

<a id="entry-i-000632"></a>

*adj.* 1. **免疫療法+的** — of or relating to immunotherapy
*“various immunotherapeutic techniques have been employed with AIDS patients”*

<a id="entry-i-000633"></a>
## immunotherapy

*n.* 1. **癌症免疫療法 /癌症免疫療法, 癌症免疫治療 /癌症免疫治療** — The treatment of cancer by improving the ability of the host to reject a tumour immunologically: cancer immunotherapy.
2. therapy designed to produce immunity to a disease or to enhance resistance by the immune system

*“Plant a thought in the students' minds”*

*“the implantation of radioactive pellets in the prostate gland”*

<a id="entry-i-000732"></a>

*v.* 1. **使生效, 貫徹, 實施, 實現, 執行** — apply in a manner consistent with its purpose or design
*“implement a procedure”*
2. **貫徹, 實施, 使生效, 執行, 實行** — ensure observance of laws and rules
*“Apply the rules to everyone”*
3. **實施 /實施, 執行 /執行, 貫徹 /貫徹, 實現 /實現, 落實 /落實** — To bring about; to put into practice; to carry out.
4. pursue to a conclusion or bring to a successful issue
*“Did he go through with the treatment?”*
*“He implemented a new economic plan”*
*n.* 1. **用具, 工具, 器具** — instrumentation (a piece of equipment or tool) used to effect an end
2. **工具** — A tool or instrument for working with.

<a id="entry-i-000733"></a>
## implementation

*“the agency was created for the implementation of the policy”*
3. the act of implementing (providing a practical means for accomplishing something); carrying into effect

<a id="entry-i-000734"></a>
## implicate

**發音：** /ˈɪmplɪkeɪt/
*v.* 1. **涉及, 涉嫌, 牽連, 連累, 使捲入** — bring into intimate and incriminating connection
*“He is implicated in the scheme to defraud the government”*
2. **暗指, 暗示** — impose, involve, or imply as a necessary accompaniment or result
*“What does this move entail?”*
3. *(archaic)* **負累 /負累, 牽涉 /牽涉** — To fold or twist together, intertwine, interlace, entangle, entwine.

<a id="entry-i-000735"></a>
## implication

**發音：** /ˌɪmpləˈkeɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **推論** — a logical relation between propositions p and q of the form `if p then q'; if p is true then q cannot be false
2. *(countable)* **含意, 意味, 含義 /含義, 涵義 /涵義** — The connective in propositional calculus that, when joining two predicates A and B in that order, has the meaning "if A is true, then B is true".
3. **意涵, 涉及, 牽連, 捲入** — a relation implicated by virtue of involvement or close connection (especially an incriminating involvement)
*“he was suspected of implication in several robberies”*
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **蘊涵 /蘊涵, 蘊含 /蘊含** — Logical consequence.
5. something that is inferred (deduced or entailed or implied)
*“his resignation had political implications”*
6. a meaning that is not expressly stated but can be inferred
*“the significance of his remark became clear only later”*
*“the expectation was spread both by word and by implication”*

<a id="entry-i-000736"></a>
## implicature

**發音：** /ɪmˈplɪkət͡ʃə/

2. **不加疑問+的, 不懷疑+的, 不提出疑問+的, 盲從+的** — being without doubt or reserve

2. **含蓄+地, 暗中+地, 暗含+地** — without ever expressing so clearly

2. *(countable)* **不受懲罰性 /不受懲罰性, 免罰 /免罰, 免於罪責 /免於罪責, 免於責罰 /免於責罰, 免於懲罰 /免於懲罰, 免於懲戒 /免於懲戒, 免於懲處 /免於懲處, 免於懲治 /免於懲治** — Exemption from punishment.
3. *(uncountable)* **有罪不罰 /有罪不罰, 有罪不訴 /有罪不訴, 有責不究 /有責不究** — Freedom from punishment or retribution; security from any reprisal or injurious consequences of an action, behaviour etc.

<a id="entry-i-000822"></a>
## impure

**發音：** /ɪmˈpjʊə(ɹ)/
*adj.* 1. **不純 /不純** — Containing undesired intermixtures
2. **不純+的, 行為不檢的, 道德敗壞的, 不純潔+的, 骯髒+的** — having a physical or moral blemish so as to make impure according to dietary or ceremonial laws
*“unclean meat”*

3. combined with extraneous elements
4. (used of persons or behaviors) immoral or obscene
*“impure thoughts”*

2. **不純, 汙染, 不潔, 摻假, 摻雜** — the condition of being impure
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **不潔 /不潔** — The condition of being impure; because of contamination, pollution, adulteration or insufficient purification.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **雜質 /雜質** — A component or additive that renders something else impure.

2. **於……中** — Surrounded by; among; amidst.

**發音：** /ˈɪnsɛns/

2. a substance that produces a fragrant odor when burned
3. the pleasing scent produced when incense is burned
*“incense filled the room”*

2. make furious

4. **事件, 事變, 事故** — a public disturbance
*“the police investigated an incident at the bus station”*

3. of a minor or subordinate nature
*“these magnificent achievements were only incidentally influenced by Oriental models”*

## incite

**發音：** /ɪnˈsaɪt/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **煽起** — To instigate a specific incident.
2. give an incentive for action
*“This moved me to sacrifice my career”*
3. provoke or stir up
*“incite a riot”*
*“set off great unrest among the people”*
4. urge on; cause to act
*“The other children egged the boy on, but he did not want to throw the stone through the window”*

<a id="entry-i-001022"></a>
## incitement

2. an act of urging on or spurring on or rousing to action or instigating
*“the incitement of mutiny”*
3. needed encouragement
*“the result was a provocation of vigorous investigation”*
4. something that incites or provokes; a means of arousing or stirring to action
5. the act of exhorting; an earnest attempt at persuasion

2. **表現無情+的, 險惡+的, 殘忍+的** — used of persons or behavior; showing no clemency or mercy

<a id="entry-i-001026"></a>
## inclination

**發音：** /ˌɪn.klɪˈneɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. **性情, 意向, 稟性, 傾向** — an attitude of mind especially one that favors one alternative over others
*“he had an inclination to give up too easily”*
*“a tendency to be too strict”*
2. **交角, 傾角, 水平差, 傾斜度** — (astronomy) the angle between the plane of the orbit and the plane of the ecliptic stated in degrees
3. **傾斜度** — (geometry) the angle formed by the x-axis and a given line (measured counterclockwise from the positive half of the x-axis)
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **傾角** — The angle of intersection of a reference plane.
5. **斜面, 斜坡** — (physics) the angle that a magnetic needle makes with the plane of the horizon
6. **傾向, 傾斜** — the property possessed by a line or surface that departs from the vertical
*“the tower had a pronounced tilt”*
*“the ship developed a list to starboard”*

<a id="entry-i-001027"></a>
## inclination of an orbit

*n.* 1. **交角, 傾角, 水平差, 傾斜度** — (astronomy) the angle between the plane of the orbit and the plane of the ecliptic stated in degrees

<a id="entry-i-001028"></a>
## incline

*v.* 1. **易於, 趨向, 傾向, 走向** — have a tendency or disposition to do or be something; be inclined
*“She tends to be nervous before her lectures”*
*“These dresses run small”*
2. **側耳傾聽, 彎腰傾聽, 躬身聽** — bend or turn (one's ear) towards a speaker in order to listen well
*“He inclined his ear to the wise old man”*
3. **躬身, 鞠躬** — lower or bend (the head or upper body), as in a nod or bow
*“She inclined her head to the student”*
4. **偏斜, 前傾, 有坡度, 傾斜** — be at an angle
*“The terrain sloped down”*
5. feel favorably disposed or willing
*“She inclines to the view that people should be allowed to expres their religious beliefs”*
6. make receptive or willing towards an action or attitude or belief
*“Their language inclines us to believe them”*
*n.* 1. **坡, 斜坡, 傾斜** — an elevated geological formation
*“he climbed the steep slope”*
*“the house was built on the side of a mountain”*
2. an inclined surface connecting two levels

<a id="entry-i-001029"></a>
## inclined

*adj.* 1. **準備好了+的** — having made preparations
*“prepared to take risks”*
2. (often followed by `to') having a preference, disposition, or tendency
*“wasn't inclined to believe the excuse”*
*“inclined to be moody”*
3. at an angle to the horizontal or vertical position
*“an inclined plane”*

<a id="entry-i-001030"></a>
## inclined plane

2. **斜面** — a simple machine for elevating objects; consists of plane surface that makes an acute angle with the horizontal

3. **傾斜計, 測斜儀** — an instrument used by surveyors in order to measure an angle of inclination or elevation

<a id="entry-i-001032"></a>

*“admit someone to the profession”*

3. **包括, 包含** — To bring into a group, class, set, or total as a (new) part or member.

*“We must include this chemical element in the group”*

<a id="entry-i-001052"></a>
## incompatibility

**發音：** /ˌɪnkəmˌpætɪˈbɪlɪti/
*n.* 1. **不一致, 矛盾** — the relation between propositions that cannot both be true at the same time
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **不相容** — The quality or state of being incompatible; inconsistency; irreconcilability.
3. **不親和性, 不相容性** — (immunology) the degree to which the body's immune system will try to reject foreign material (as transfused blood or transplanted tissue)
4. the quality of being unable to exist or work in congenial combination

<a id="entry-i-001053"></a>
## incompatible

**發音：** /ˌɪŋkəmˈpætɪbəl/
*adj.* 1. **不兼容的, 不相容的** — Of two or more things: that cannot coexist; not congruous because of differences; unable to function together due to dissimilarities.
2. **反作用+的, 相剋+的, 相沖突+的, 起反作用+的** — used especially of drugs or muscles that counteract or neutralize each other's effect
3. **不相容+的, 對比+的** — of words so related that one contrasts with the other
*“`rich' and `hard-up' are contrastive terms”*
4. not compatible
*“incompatible personalities”*
*“incompatible colors”*
5. not suitable to your tastes or needs
*“the uncongenial roommates were always fighting”*
*“the task was uncongenial to one sensitive to rebuffs”*
6. incapable of being used with or connected to other devices or components without modification

<a id="entry-i-001054"></a>
## incompetence

2. lack of physical or intellectual ability or qualifications

<a id="entry-i-001055"></a>
## incompetent

*n.* 1. someone who is not competent to take effective action
*adj.* 1. **拙劣+的** — showing lack of skill or aptitude
*“a bungling workman”*
*“did a clumsy job”*
2. **不稱職+的, 不勝任+的, 無能力+的, 不適當+的, 無能+的** — not meeting requirements
*“unequal to the demands put upon him”*
3. legally not qualified or sufficient
*“a wife is usually considered unqualified to testify against her husband”*
*“incompetent witnesses”*
4. not qualified or suited for a purpose
*“an incompetent secret service”*
*“the filming was hopeless incompetent”*
5. not doing a good job
*“incompetent at chess”*

## increase

**發音：** /ɪnˈkɹiːs/
*v.* 1. **加大, 增長, 增大, 增加** — become bigger or greater in amount
*“The amount of work increased”*
2. *(intransitive)* **增加, 增長 /增長, 增大** — (of a quantity, etc.) To become larger or greater, to greaten.
3. *(transitive)* **增加, 增長 /增長, 增大** — To make (a quantity, etc.) larger.
4. make bigger or more
*“The boss finally increased her salary”*
*“The university increased the number of students it admitted”*
*n.* 1. **增加量** — a quantity that is added
*“there was an addition to property taxes this year”*
*“they recorded the cattle's gain in weight over a period of weeks”*
2. **增值, 增量, 增多, 增大, 增加** — a process of becoming larger or longer or more numerous or more important
*“the increase in unemployment”*
*“the growth of population”*
3. **增額, 增量, 增加** — the amount by which something increases
*“they proposed an increase of 15 percent in the fare”*
4. **加大, 增大, 增加** — the act of increasing something
*“he gave me an increase in salary”*
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **增加, 增大, 增長 /增長, 增幅** — An amount by which a quantity is increased.
6. *(countable, uncountable)* **增加, 增大** — For a quantity, the act or process of becoming larger.

*“The female covers the eggs”*

## indeclinable

**發音：** /ˌɪndɪˈklɑjnəb(ə)l/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **不變格的 /不變格的** — Not grammatically declinable; having no inflections.

<a id="entry-i-001124"></a>
## indeed

**發音：** /ɪnˈdiːd/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **的確 /的確, 確實 /確實, 事實上 /事實上, 實際上 /實際上** — In fact.
2. in truth (often tends to intensify)
*“they said the car would break down and indeed it did”*
*“it is very cold indeed”*
3. (used as an interjection) an expression of surprise or skepticism or irony etc.
*“Wants to marry the butler? Indeed!”*
*interj.* 1. **就是, 確實 /確實** — Indicates agreement with another speaker's previous statement.

<a id="entry-i-001125"></a>
## indefatigable

**發音：** /ˌɪn.dɪˈfæt.ɪ.ɡə.bəl/
*adj.* 1. **不倦** — Extremely persistent and untiring.
2. showing sustained enthusiastic action with unflagging vitality
*“an indefatigable advocate of equal rights”*
*“a tireless worker”*

<a id="entry-i-001126"></a>
## indefensible

*adj.* 1. **不能防衛+的, 不能防守+的, 無可辯解的, 無法辯護的, 無辯護餘地+的** — (of theories etc) incapable of being defended or justified
2. not able to be protected against attack
3. incapable of being justified or explained

<a id="entry-i-001127"></a>
## indefinite

**發音：** /ɪnˈdɛfɪnɪt/
*adj.* 1. **不定** — Being an integral without specified limits.
2. vague or not clearly defined or stated
*“must you be so indefinite?”*
*“amorphous blots of color having vague and indefinite edges”*
3. not decided or not known
*“were indefinite about their plans”*
*“plans are indefinite”*

<a id="entry-i-001128"></a>
## indefinite article

*n.* 1. **不定冠詞 /不定冠詞** — A word preceding a noun to indicate that the noun refers to any member of the class of objects named by the noun.
2. a determiner (as `a' or `some' in English) that indicates nonspecific reference

<a id="entry-i-001129"></a>
## indefinite integral

*n.* 1. **不定積分 /不定積分** — A function whose derivative is a given function; an antiderivative.
2. the set of functions F(x) + C, where C is any real number, such that F(x) is the integral of f(x)

<a id="entry-i-001130"></a>
## indefinite pronoun

*n.* 1. **不定代名詞 /不定代名詞** — A pronoun that does not specify the identity of its referent.

<a id="entry-i-001131"></a>
## indefinite quantity

*n.* 1. **不確定值, 估計量** — an estimated quantity

<a id="entry-i-001132"></a>
## indegree

**發音：** /ˈɪndəˌɡɹiː/
*n.* 1. **入度, 入次數** — The number of edges directed into a vertex in a directed graph

<a id="entry-i-001133"></a>
## indehiscent

*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **不開裂的 /不開裂的** — Not dehiscing when mature; not dehiscent.
2. (of e.g. fruits) not opening spontaneously at maturity to release seeds

<a id="entry-i-001134"></a>
## indelibly

*adv.* 1. **不可磨滅地, 不能消除+地, 去不掉+地** — in an indelible manner
*“this tradition has left its mark indelibly upon the social, political, educational and industrial fabric of this country”*

<a id="entry-i-001135"></a>
## indelicate

*adj.* 1. **不文雅+的, 粗俗的** — in violation of good taste even verging on the indecent
*“an indelicate remark”*
*“an off-color joke”*
2. lacking propriety and good taste in manners and conduct
*“indecorous behavior”*
3. verging on the indecent
*“an indelicate proposition”*

<a id="entry-i-001136"></a>
## indemnification

*n.* 1. **補償, 補償金, 賠償, 賠償金** — a sum of money paid in compensation for loss or injury
2. an act of compensation for actual loss or damage or for trouble and annoyance

<a id="entry-i-001137"></a>
## indemnify

*v.* 1. **保障, 保護** — secure against future loss, damage, or liability; give security for
*“This plan indemnifies workers against wages lost through illness”*
2. **賠償, 補償** — make amends for; pay compensation for
*“One can never fully repair the suffering and losses of the Jews in the Third Reich”*
*“She was compensated for the loss of her arm in the accident”*

<a id="entry-i-001138"></a>
## indemnity

**發音：** /ɪnˈdɛmnɪti/
*n.* 1. **保障, 保險** — protection against future loss
2. **免罰, 免除賠償責任, 赦免** — legal exemption from liability for damages
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **賠款 /賠款** — Repayment; compensation for loss or injury.
4. **補償, 補償金, 賠償, 賠償金** — a sum of money paid in compensation for loss or injury

<a id="entry-i-001139"></a>
## indene

*n.* 1. **茚** — The bicyclic hydrocarbon consisting of a benzene ring fused to one of cyclopentadiene
2. **茚** — a colorless liquid hydrocarbon extracted from petroleum or coal tar and used in making synthetic resins

<a id="entry-i-001140"></a>
## indentation

**發音：** /ˌɪndɛnˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **凹入處, 凹口, 壓痕, 凹痕** — a concave cut into a surface or edge (as in a coastline)
2. **造成坑洞** — the formation of small pits in a surface as a consequence of corrosion
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **縮進 /縮進** — The act of beginning a line or series of lines at a little distance within the flush line of the column or page, as in the common way of beginning the first line of a paragraph.
4. **使呈鋸齒形, 缺刻** — the act of cutting into an edge with toothlike notches or angular incisions
5. the space left between the margin and the start of an indented line

<a id="entry-i-001141"></a>
## indenture

*n.* 1. **凹入處, 凹口, 壓痕, 凹痕** — a concave cut into a surface or edge (as in a coastline)
2. formal agreement between the issuer of bonds and the bondholders as to terms of the debt
3. a contract binding one party into the service of another for a specified term
4. the space left between the margin and the start of an indented line
*v.* 1. bind by or as if by indentures, as of an apprentice or servant
*“an indentured servant”*

<a id="entry-i-001142"></a>
## indentured

*adj.* 1. **以契約束縛+的, 受約束+的, 被束縛+的** — bound by contract

<a id="entry-i-001143"></a>
## indentured servant

*n.* 1. *(historical)* **契約傭工 /契約傭工, 契約傭僕 /契約傭僕, 契約奴 /契約奴, 白奴** — A debt bondage worker who is under contract of an employer for a specified period of time, in exchange for transportation, food, drink, clothing, lodging, and other necessities.

<a id="entry-i-001144"></a>
## independence

**發音：** /ˌɪn.dɪˈpɛn.dəns/
*n.* 1. **獨立, 自主** — freedom from control or influence of another or others
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **獨立 /獨立** — The quality or state of being independent; lack of dependence; the state of not being reliant on, or controlled by, others.
3. the successful ending of the American Revolution
*“they maintained close relations with England even after independence”*
4. a city in western Missouri; the beginning of the Santa Fe Trail

<a id="entry-i-001145"></a>
## Independence Day

<a id="entry-i-001146"></a>
## independence day

*n.* 1. **獨立日 /獨立日, 獨立紀念日 /獨立紀念日** — An annual celebration commemorating the anniversary of a nation's assumption of independent statehood.
2. **獨立日, 獨立紀念日** — a legal holiday in the United States

<a id="entry-i-001147"></a>
## independence hall

*n.* 1. **獨立大廳** — the building in Philadelphia where the Declaration of Independence was signed

<a id="entry-i-001148"></a>
## independency

*n.* 1. **獨立, 自主** — freedom from control or influence of another or others

<a id="entry-i-001149"></a>
## independent

**發音：** /ˌɪn.dɪˈpɛn.dənt/
*adj.* 1. **不須依賴+的, 獨立+的, 不受約束+的** — free from external control and constraint
*“an independent mind”*
*“a series of independent judgments”*
2. **獨立 /獨立** — Not dependent; not contingent or depending on something else; free.
3. **無黨籍 /無黨籍** — Not affiliated with any political party.
4. (of political bodies) not controlled by outside forces
*“an autonomous judiciary”*
*“a sovereign state”*
5. (of a clause) capable of standing syntactically alone as a complete sentence
*“the main (or independent) clause in a complex sentence has at least a subject and a verb”*
6. not controlled by a party or interest group
*n.* 1. a neutral or uncommitted person (especially in politics)
2. a writer or artist who sells services to different employers without a long-term contract with any of them

<a id="entry-i-001150"></a>
## independent contractor

*n.* 1. **獨立承包人 /獨立承包人, 獨立承包商 /獨立承包商** — A person working independently, under a contract; a self-employed person.

<a id="entry-i-001151"></a>

*prop. n.* 1. **巴布亞新幾內亞獨立國 /巴布亞新幾內亞獨立國** — Official name of Papua New Guinea: an island country in Oceania.

<a id="entry-i-001152"></a>
## independentize

*v.* 1. **獨立化 /獨立化** — To make independent.

<a id="entry-i-001153"></a>

**發音：** /ˌɪndɪˈpɛndəntli/
*adv.* 1. **獨立地 /獨立地, 自立地** — In an independent manner.
2. on your own; without outside help
*“the children worked on the project independently”*
3. apart from others
*“the clothes were hung severally”*

<a id="entry-i-001154"></a>
## indescribable

**發音：** /ɪndɪˈskɹaɪbəbl̩/
*adj.* 1. **無法形容 /無法形容, 不可名狀 /不可名狀** — Impossible (or very difficult) to describe.
2. defying expression or description
*“indefinable yearnings”*
*“indescribable beauty”*

<a id="entry-i-001155"></a>
## indescribably

*adv.* 1. **難以形容地 /難以形容地, 無法描述地 /無法描述地** — In an indescribable manner.
2. **不可名狀+地, 無法形容+地, 說不出+地, 難以形容+地, 難以言喻+地** — to an inexpressible degree
*“she was looking very young tonight, and, as usual, indescribably beautiful, in a simple strapless dress of a green and white silky cotton”*

*“their relationship was of an indeterminably vague nature”*

<a id="entry-i-001158"></a>
## index

**發音：** /ˈɪndɛks/
*n.* 1. **指數, 指標** — a numerical scale used to compare variables with one another or with some reference number
2. **索引, 引得** — An alphabetical listing of items and their location.
3. **索引** — an alphabetical listing of names and topics along with page numbers where they are discussed
4. **食指** — the finger next to the thumb
5. **指數 /指數** — A single number calculated from an array of prices or of quantities.
6. a number or ratio (a value on a scale of measurement) derived from a series of observed facts; can reveal relative changes as a function of time
*v.* 1. list in an index
2. provide with an index
*“index the book”*
3. adjust through indexation
*“The government indexes wages and prices”*

## index finger

*n.* 1. **食指** — the finger next to the thumb

<a id="entry-i-001214"></a>
## indicant

*n.* 1. **標誌** — something that serves to indicate or suggest
*“an indication of foul play”*
*“indications of strain”*
2. a number or ratio (a value on a scale of measurement) derived from a series of observed facts; can reveal relative changes as a function of time

<a id="entry-i-001215"></a>
## indicate

**發音：** /ˈɪndɪˌkeɪt/
*v.* 1. **指, 指出, 表明, 指示, 表示** — indicate a place, direction, person, or thing; either spatially or figuratively
*“I showed the customer the glove section”*
*“He pointed to the empty parking space”*
2. **指明, 指示, 證明** — give evidence of
*“The evidence argues for your claim”*
*“The results indicate the need for more work”*
3. *(sometimes, transitive)* **表明, 表示, 指示, 預示 /預示** — To point out; to discover; to direct to a knowledge of; to show; to make known.
4. *(transitive)* **表示, 暗示** — To show or manifest by symptoms.
5. *(transitive)* **指示** — To signal in a vehicle the desire to turn right or left.
6. *(transitive)* **指示** — To investigate the condition or power of, as of steam engine, by means of an indicator.

<a id="entry-i-001216"></a>
## indication

**發音：** /ɪndɪˈkeɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **標誌** — something that serves to indicate or suggest
*“an indication of foul play”*
*“indications of strain”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **指示** — A fact that shows that something exists or may happen.
3. *(countable, rare, uncountable)* **符號 /符號** — A mark or another symbol used to represent something.
4. the act of indicating or pointing out by name
5. (medicine) a reason to prescribe a drug or perform a procedure
*“the presence of bacterial infection was an indication for the use of antibiotics”*
6. something (as a course of action) that is indicated as expedient or necessary
*“there were indications that it was time to leave”*

<a id="entry-i-001217"></a>
## indicative

**發音：** /ɪnˈdɪk.ə.tɪv/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **陳述語氣** — The indicative mood.
2. **直陳語氣, 陳述語氣** — a mood (grammatically unmarked) that represents the act or state as an objective fact
*adj.* 1. **直陳+的, 陳述+的** — relating to the mood of verbs that is used simple in declarative statements
*“indicative mood”*
2. **指示+的, 提示性+的, 表示+的, 預示的** — (usually followed by `of') pointing out or revealing clearly
*“actions indicative of fear”*

<a id="entry-i-001218"></a>
## indicative mood

*n.* 1. **直陳語氣 /直陳語氣** — The mood of a verb used in ordinary, factual, or objective statements.
2. **直陳語氣, 陳述語氣** — a mood (grammatically unmarked) that represents the act or state as an objective fact

<a id="entry-i-001219"></a>
## indicator

**發音：** /ˈɪn.dɪˌkeɪ.tə(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. **記錄器, 指示器** — a device for showing the operating condition of some system
2. **指示符** — A pointer or index that indicates something.
3. **指針 /指針** — The needle or dial on such a meter.
4. *(Australia, Ireland, New-Zealand)* **方向燈 /方向燈, 指揮燈 /指揮燈, 方向指示燈 /方向指示燈, 轉向燈 /轉向燈** — A turn signal; each of the flashing lights on each side of a vehicle which indicate a turn is being made to left or right, or a lane change etc.
5. a number or ratio (a value on a scale of measurement) derived from a series of observed facts; can reveal relative changes as a function of time
6. a signal for attracting attention

<a id="entry-i-001220"></a>
## indicatory

*adj.* 1. **指示+的, 提示性+的, 表示+的, 預示的** — (usually followed by `of') pointing out or revealing clearly
*“actions indicative of fear”*

3. *(transitive)* **起訴 /起訴** — To make a formal accusation or indictment for a crime against (a party) by the findings of a jury, especially a grand jury.

<a id="entry-i-001226"></a>
## indifference

**發音：** /ɪnˈdɪf.ɹəns/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **不關心 /不關心, 冷淡** — The state of being indifferent.
2. unbiased impartial unconcern
3. apathy demonstrated by an absence of emotional reactions
4. the trait of lacking enthusiasm for or interest in things generally
5. the trait of remaining calm and seeming not to care; a casual lack of concern

<a id="entry-i-001227"></a>
## indifferent

**發音：** /ɪnˈdɪf.ɹənt/
*adj.* 1. **不感興趣+的** — marked by a lack of interest
*“an apathetic audience”*
*“the universe is neither hostile nor friendly; it is simply indifferent"”*
2. **冷漠, 冷淡** — Ambivalent; unconcerned; uninterested, apathetic.
3. **一般的, 不好+的, 中等+的, 平庸+的, 較差+的** — fairly poor to not very good
*“has an indifferent singing voice”*
*“has indifferent qualifications for the job”*
4. showing no care or concern in attitude or action
*“indifferent to the sufferings of others”*
*“indifferent to her plea”*
5. (usually followed by `to') unwilling or refusing to pay heed
*“deaf to her warnings”*
6. (often followed by `to') lacking importance; not mattering one way or the other
*“whether you choose to do it or not is a matter that is quite immaterial (or indifferent)”*
*“what others think is altogether indifferent to him”*

<a id="entry-i-001228"></a>
## indifferently

*“she shrugged indifferently”*

<a id="entry-i-001244"></a>
## indirect

**發音：** /ˌɪn.daɪˈɹɛkt/
*adj.* 1. **間接 /間接** — Not direct
2. **不坦率+的, 不直接了當+的** — extended senses; not direct in manner or language or behavior or action
*“making indirect but legitimate inquiries”*
*“an indirect insult”*
3. having intervening factors or persons or influences
*“reflection from the ceiling provided a soft indirect light”*
*“indirect evidence”*
4. not direct in spatial dimension; not leading by a straight line or course to a destination
*“sometimes taking an indirect path saves time”*
*“you must take an indirect course in sailing”*
5. descended from a common ancestor but through different lines
*“cousins are collateral relatives”*
*“an indirect descendant of the Stuarts”*
6. not as a direct effect or consequence
*“indirect benefits”*
*“an indirect advantage”*

<a id="entry-i-001245"></a>

<a id="entry-i-001246"></a>
## indirect lighting

*n.* 1. **間接照明** — a concealed lighting fixture

<a id="entry-i-001247"></a>
## indirect object

2. the object that is the recipient or beneficiary of the action of the verb

<a id="entry-i-001248"></a>
## indirect tax

*n.* 1. **間接稅, 間接稅收** — a tax levied on goods or services rather than on persons or organizations

<a id="entry-i-001252"></a>

*adj.* 1. **不加區別的 /不加區別的, 不加選擇的 /不加選擇的, 不加鑒別的 /不加鑑別的, 不分好壞的 /不分好壞的** — Without care in making distinctions, thoughtless.

<a id="entry-i-001254"></a>
## indispensable

*adj.* 1. **必要的** — Absolutely necessary or requisite; that one cannot do without.
2. *(obsolete)* **必不可少的** — Not admitting ecclesiastical dispensation; not subject to release or exemption; that cannot be allowed by bending the canonical rules.
3. not to be dispensed with; essential
*“foods indispensable to good nutrition”*

*“antipathetic to new ideas”*

<a id="entry-i-001263"></a>
## individual

*“there was too much for one person to do”*
2. **個人, 個體** — a single organism
3. **個體 /個體, 個人 /個人, 私人** — A person considered alone, rather than as belonging to a group of people.
*adj.* 1. **個體+的, 個人+的, 個別+的, 單獨+的** — characteristic of or meant for a single person or thing
*“an individual serving”*
*“single occupancy”*
2. **單個 /單個, 獨個 /獨個, 個別 /個別** — Relating to a single person or thing as opposed to more than one.
3. being or characteristic of a single thing or person
*“individual drops of rain”*
*“please mark the individual pages”*
4. separate and distinct from others of the same kind
*“mark the individual pages”*
*“on a case-by-case basis”*
5. concerning one person exclusively
*“we all have individual cars”*

*“The sounds were individualized by sharpness and tone”*

<a id="entry-i-001265"></a>
## individualism

**發音：** /ˌɪn.dɪˈvɪ.dʒu.əˌlɪ.zəm/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **個人主義 /個人主義** — The tendency for a person to act without reference to others, particularly in matters of style, fashion or mode of thought.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **個人主義 /個人主義** — The moral stance, political philosophy, or social outlook that promotes independence and self-reliance of individual people, while opposing the interference with each person's choices by society, the state, or any other group or institution.
3. the quality of being individual
*“so absorbed by the movement that she lost all sense of individuality”*
4. a belief in the importance of the individual and the virtue of self-reliance and personal independence
5. the doctrine that government should not interfere in commercial affairs

<a id="entry-i-001266"></a>

<a id="entry-i-001312"></a>
## induct

*v.* 1. **使就職, 使正式就任** — place ceremoniously or formally in an office or position
*“there was a ceremony to induct the president of the Academy”*
2. **引入, 使入門** — accept people into an exclusive society or group, usually with some rite
*“African men are initiated when they reach puberty”*
3. **使入門, 使正式入會, 使正式參加, 引入** — admit as a member
*“We were inducted into the honor society”*
4. produce electric current by electrostatic or magnetic processes
5. introduce or initiate
*“The young geisha was inducted into the ways of her profession”*

<a id="entry-i-001313"></a>
## inductance

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電感** — The property of an electric circuit by which a voltage is induced in it by a changing magnetic field.
2. **感應器** — an electrical device (typically a conducting coil) that introduces inductance into a circuit
3. an electrical phenomenon whereby an electromotive force (EMF) is generated in a closed circuit by a change in the flow of current

<a id="entry-i-001314"></a>
## inductance unit

*n.* 1. **電感單位** — a measure of the property of an electric circuit by which an electromotive force is induced in it

<a id="entry-i-001315"></a>
## induction

**發音：** /ɪnˈdʌkʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **感應 /感應** — Generation of an electric current by a varying magnetic field.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **歸納 /歸納** — Derivation of general principles from specific instances.
3. a formal entry into an organization or position or office
*“his initiation into the club”*
*“he was ordered to report for induction into the army”*
4. an electrical phenomenon whereby an electromotive force (EMF) is generated in a closed circuit by a change in the flow of current
5. reasoning from detailed facts to general principles
6. stimulation that calls up (draws forth) a particular class of behaviors
*“the elicitation of his testimony was not easy”*

<a id="entry-i-001316"></a>
## induction coil

*n.* 1. **感應線圈** — a coil for producing a high voltage from a low-voltage source

<a id="entry-i-001317"></a>
## induction heating

*n.* 1. **感應加熱** — the heating of a conducting material caused by an electric current induced in it

<a id="entry-i-001318"></a>
## inductive

*adj.* 1. **感應+的, 感生+的, 電感+的** — arising from inductance
*“inductive reactance”*
2. of reasoning; proceeding from particular facts to a general conclusion
*“inductive reasoning”*
3. inducing or influencing; leading on
*“inductive to the sin of Eve"- John Milton”*

<a id="entry-i-001319"></a>
## inductor

*“She indulges in ice cream”*

<a id="entry-i-001414"></a>
## infect

**發音：** /ɪnˈfɛkt/
*v.* 1. **傳染, 侵染, 感染** — communicate a disease to
*“Your children have infected you with this head cold”*
2. *(transitive)* **傳染 /傳染, 感染** — To bring (the body or part of it) into contact with a substance that causes illness (a pathogen), so that the pathogen begins to act on the body; (of a pathogen) to come into contact with (a body or body part) and begin to act on it.
3. *(transitive)* **感染** — To make somebody enthusiastic about one's own passion, or to communicate a feeling to others, or a feeling communicating itself to others.
4. **使受影響, 使腐化, 侵染, 腐蝕** — corrupt with ideas or an ideology
*“society was infected by racism”*
5. contaminate with a disease or microorganism
6. affect in a contagious way
*“His laughter infects everyone who is in the same room”*

## infected

## infection

*n.* 1. **侵染, 感染, 傳染** — the pathological state resulting from the invasion of the body by pathogenic microorganisms
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **感染** — An uncontrolled growth of harmful microorganisms in a host.
3. **傳染, 侵染, 感染, 被感染** — (medicine) the invasion of the body by pathogenic microorganisms and their multiplication which can lead to tissue damage and disease
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **傳染 /傳染** — The act or process of infecting.
5. **傳染, 傳染病** — an incident in which an infectious disease is transmitted
6. (phonetics) the alteration of a speech sound under the influence of a neighboring sound

<a id="entry-i-001432"></a>

<a id="entry-i-001433"></a>
## inferior orbital fissure

<a id="entry-i-001434"></a>

*n.* 1. **巖下竇 /巖下竇, 下巖竇 /下巖竇** — The larger of the two petrosal sinuses that is situated inferiorly in a sulcus on each side formed by the junction of the petrous portion of the temporal bone and the basilar portion of the occipital bone and that extends from the posterior inferior end of the cavernous sinus through the jugular foramen to join the internal jugular vein of the same side.

<a id="entry-i-001435"></a>

<a id="entry-i-001436"></a>
## inferior rectus

<a id="entry-i-001437"></a>

<a id="entry-i-001438"></a>
