*“the invagination of the blastula”*

<a id="entry-i-001500"></a>
## inform

**發音：** /ɪnˈfɔɹm/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **通知, 告知** — To communicate knowledge to.
2. impart knowledge of some fact, state or affairs, or event to
*“I informed him of his rights”*
3. give character or essence to
*“The principles that inform modern teaching”*
4. act as an informer
*“She had informed on her own parents for years”*

<a id="entry-i-001501"></a>
## informal

**發音：** /ɪnˈfɔː.m(ə)l/
*adj.* 1. **不拘禮+的, 非正式+的** — not formal
*“conservative people unaccustomed to informal dress”*
*“an informal free-and-easy manner”*
2. **非正式** — Not formal or ceremonious.
3. **非正式** — Not in accord with the usual regulations.
4. **俗** — Reflecting everyday, non-ceremonious usage.
5. not officially recognized or controlled
*“an informal agreement”*
*“a loose organization of the local farmers”*
6. used of spoken and written language

<a id="entry-i-001502"></a>
## informant

**發音：** /ɪnˈfɔːmənt/
*n.* 1. **告密者, 提供消息的人, 線人** — a person who supplies information
2. **提供消息的人, 告密者, 線人 /線人, 舉報人 /舉報人, 檢舉人 /檢舉人** — One who relays confidential information to someone, especially to the police; an informer.
3. someone who sees an event and reports what happened

<a id="entry-i-001503"></a>
## informatics

**發音：** /ɪnfəˈmætɪks/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **信息學 /信息學, 資訊學 /資訊學, 情報學 /情報學** — A branch of information science and of computer science that focuses on the study of information processing, particularly with respect to systems integration and human interactions with machine and data.
2. the sciences concerned with gathering, manipulating, storing, retrieving, and classifying recorded information

<a id="entry-i-001504"></a>
## information

**發音：** /ˌɪn.fəˈmeɪ.ʃn̩/
*n.* 1. **信息** — a message received and understood
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **信息, 資訊 /資訊, 情報 /情報** — Things that are or can be known about a given topic; communicable knowledge of something.
3. knowledge acquired through study or experience or instruction
4. formal accusation of a crime
5. a collection of facts from which conclusions may be drawn
*“statistical data”*
6. (communication theory) a numerical measure of the uncertainty of an outcome
*“the signal contained thousands of bits of information”*

<a id="entry-i-001505"></a>
## Information Age

*prop. n.* 1. **信息時代 /信息時代** — The current era, characterised by the increasing importance and availability of information (especially by means of computers), as opposed to previous eras (such as the Industrial Age) in which most endeavours related to some physical process or product.

<a id="entry-i-001506"></a>
## information age

*n.* 1. **信息時代** — a period beginning in the last quarter of the 20th century when information became easily accessible through publications and through the manipulation of information by computers and computer networks

<a id="entry-i-001507"></a>
## information desk

*n.* 1. **詢問檯 /詢問臺, 服務檯 /服務檯** — A staffed location providing information to the public, especially to visitors or tourists.

<a id="entry-i-001508"></a>
## information entropy

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **信息熵, 資訊熵 /資訊熵** — A measure of the uncertainty associated with a random variable; a measure of the average information content one is missing when one does not know the value of the random variable (usually in units such as bits); the amount of information (measured in, say, bits) contained per average instance of a character in a stream of characters.

<a id="entry-i-001509"></a>
## information literacy

*n.* 1. **信息素養 /信息素養, 資訊素養 /資訊素養** — The overall understanding of how information is produced and valued, and used to create new knowledge.

<a id="entry-i-001510"></a>
## information measure

*n.* 1. **信息度量** — a system of measurement of information based on the probabilities of the events that convey information

<a id="entry-i-001511"></a>
## information processing system

*n.* 1. **電子計算機, 電腦, 計算機** — a machine for performing calculations automatically

<a id="entry-i-001512"></a>
## information retrieval

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **信息檢索 /信息檢索, 資訊檢索 /資訊檢索** — The science of searching for information in documents, searching for documents themselves, searching for metadata which describe documents, or searching within databases, whether relational stand-alone databases or hypertextually-networked databases such as the World Wide Web.

<a id="entry-i-001513"></a>
## information science

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **信息科學 /信息科學, 信息學 /信息學, 情報學 /情報學, 資訊科學 /資訊科學, 資訊學 /資訊學** — A broad and interdisciplinary science that deals with the gathering, classification, storage, manipulation, retrieval and analysis of information as an organized resource.
2. the sciences concerned with gathering, manipulating, storing, retrieving, and classifying recorded information

<a id="entry-i-001514"></a>
## information security

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **信息安全** — The protection of information and information systems from unauthorized access and disruption.

<a id="entry-i-001515"></a>
## information society

*n.* 1. **信息社會 /信息社會, 資訊社會 /資訊社會** — A society where the creation, distribution, use, integration and manipulation of information is a significant economic, political, and cultural activity.

<a id="entry-i-001516"></a>
## information symmetry

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **信息對稱 /信息對稱** — A condition in which all relevant information is known to all parties involved. For example, in the stock market, stock information has a full public disclosure, and all investors are in the same position to share information.

<a id="entry-i-001517"></a>
## information system

*n.* 1. **信息系統 /信息系統, 資訊系統 /資訊系統** — Any data processing system, either manual or computerized.
2. **信息系統** — system consisting of the network of all communication channels used within an organization
3. **信息系統 /信息系統, 資訊系統 /資訊系統** — Any telecommunications system whose primary function is to transmit and/or display data.

<a id="entry-i-001518"></a>
## information technology

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **信息技術 /信息技術, 資訊技術 /資訊技術, 情報技術 /情報技術, 資訊科技 /資訊科技** — The practice of creating and/or studying computer systems and applications.
2. the branch of engineering that deals with the use of computers and telecommunications to retrieve and store and transmit information

<a id="entry-i-001519"></a>
## information theory

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **資訊理論 /資訊理論, 信息論 /信息論** — A branch of applied mathematics and engineering involving the quantification of information sent over a communication channel, disregarding the meaning of the sent messages, exemplified by the noisy-channel coding theorem.
2. (computer science) a statistical theory dealing with the limits and efficiency of information processing

<a id="entry-i-001520"></a>
## information warfare

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **信息戰 /信息戰, 資訊戰 /資訊戰** — The use and management of information in pursuit of an advantage over an opponent, such as propaganda, disinformation, and gathering assurances that one's own information is accurate.
2. the use of information or information technology during a time of crisis or conflict to achieve or promote specific objectives over a specific adversary or adversaries
*“not everyone agrees that information warfare is limited to the realm of traditional warfare”*

<a id="entry-i-001521"></a>
## informational rna

*n.* 1. **信使核糖核酸, 模板核糖核酸** — the template for protein synthesis; the form of RNA that carries information from DNA in the nucleus to the ribosome sites of protein synthesis in the cell

<a id="entry-i-001522"></a>
## informatively

*adv.* 1. **使知道消息+地, 增進知識+地, 教育+地, 有益+地** — in an informative manner

<a id="entry-i-001523"></a>
## informed consent

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **知情同意** — Consent to an action by another person given based upon a clear appreciation and understanding of the facts, implications, and consequences of the action.
2. consent by a patient to undergo a medical or surgical treatment or to participate in an experiment after the patient understands the risks involved

<a id="entry-i-001524"></a>
## infract

*v.* 1. **犯罪, 違法** — act in disregard of laws, rules, contracts, or promises
*“offend all laws of humanity”*
*“violate the basic laws or human civilization”*

<a id="entry-i-001525"></a>
## infraction

**發音：** /ɪnˈfɹakʃən/
*n.* 1. **違規 /違規** — A minor offence, petty crime.
2. **輕罪, 違反** — a crime less serious than a felony
3. **違規 /違規** — A violation; breach.

**發音：** /ˌɪn.fɹəˈhaɪˌɔɪd/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable, relational)* **舌骨下** — Below the hyoid bone in the neck.

<a id="entry-i-001527"></a>

*adj.* 1. **上頜下+的, 顎+的** — relating to the lower jaw

<a id="entry-i-001528"></a>
## infraorbital

**發音：** /ˌɪn.fɹəˈɔɹ.bət.l̩/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable, relational)* **眶下** — Below the orbit of the eye.

<a id="entry-i-001529"></a>
## infraorbital artery

*n.* 1. **眶下動脈 /眶下動脈** — A branch or continuation of the maxillary artery that runs along the infraorbital groove with the infraorbital nerve and passes through the infraorbital foramen to give off branches which supply the face just below the eye.
2. an artery that originates from the maxillary artery and supplies structures below the orbit (from lower eyelid to upper lip)

<a id="entry-i-001530"></a>
## infraorbital foramen

*n.* 1. **眶下孔** — An opening in the maxilla just below the lower rim of the orbit that gives passage to the infraorbital artery, nerve, and vein.

<a id="entry-i-001531"></a>
## infraorbital groove

*n.* 1. **眶下溝 /眶下溝** — A groove in the middle of the posterior part of the bony floor of the orbit that gives passage to the infraorbital artery, vein, and nerve.

<a id="entry-i-001532"></a>
## infraorder

**發音：** /ˈɪn.fɹəˌɔː.də/
*n.* 1. **下目** — A taxonomic category below suborder and above superfamily.

<a id="entry-i-001533"></a>
## infrared

**發音：** /ˌɪnfɹəˈɹɛd/
*adj.* 1. **紅外, 紅外線+的** — having or employing wavelengths longer than light but shorter than radio waves; lying outside the visible spectrum at its red end
*“infrared radiation”*
*“infrared photography”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **紅外 /紅外** — Having a wavelength in the infrared spectrum.
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **紅外線 /紅外線** — The electromagnetic radiation of a wavelength longer than visible light, but shorter than microwave radiation, having a wavelength between 700 nm and 1 mm.
2. the infrared region of the electromagnetic spectrum; electromagnetic wave frequencies below the visible range
*“they could sense radiation in the infrared”*
3. electromagnetic radiation with wavelengths longer than visible light but shorter than radio waves

<a id="entry-i-001534"></a>
## infrared lamp

*n.* 1. **紅外熱燈** — electric heater consisting of a high-power incandescent lamp that emits infrared radiation
*“the bathroom could be warmed by an infrared lamp”*

<a id="entry-i-001535"></a>
## infrared radiation

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **紅外線 /紅外線** — Electromagnetic radiation having a wavelength approximately between 1 micrometre and 1 millimetre; perceived as heat.
2. electromagnetic radiation with wavelengths longer than visible light but shorter than radio waves

<a id="entry-i-001536"></a>
## infrasound

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **次聲波, 次聲** — Sound waves having a frequency below the human audible range (below about 20 Hz).

<a id="entry-i-001537"></a>
## infrastructure

**發音：** /ˈɪnfɹəˌstɹʌkt͡ʃə/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **基礎設施 /基礎設施, 基建** — The facilities, services and installations needed for the functioning of a community or society.
2. **基建, 基礎設施** — the stock of basic facilities and capital equipment needed for the functioning of a country or area
*“the industrial base of Japan”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **基礎設施 /基礎設施** — An underlying base or foundation for a building, organization, or system.
4. **下部構造, 基礎設施, 底部構造** — the basic structure or features of a system or organization

<a id="entry-i-001538"></a>
## infratemporal

**發音：** /ˌɪn.fɹəˈtɛm.pəɹ.əl/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **顳下 /顳下** — Below the temple or temporal bone.

<a id="entry-i-001539"></a>
## infratemporal crest

*n.* 1. **顳下嵴 /顳下嵴** — A transverse ridge on the outer surface of the greater wing of the sphenoid bone that divides it into a superior portion that contributes to the formation of the temporal fossa and an inferior portion that contributes to the formation of the infratemporal fossa.

<a id="entry-i-001540"></a>
## infratemporal fossa

*n.* 1. **顳下窩 /顳下窩** — A fossa that is bounded above by the plane of the zygomatic arch, laterally by the ramus of the mandible, and medially by the pterygoid plate, and that contains the masseter and pterygoid muscles and the mandibular nerve.

*“in your 1850 church you not infrequently find a dramatic contrast between the sumptuous appointments of the building itself and the inhuman barrack-like living conditions in the church room”*

*“He ran afoul of the law”*
*“This behavior conflicts with our rules”*
2. **侵害, 侵犯** — advance beyond the usual limit

<a id="entry-i-001544"></a>
## infringement

*n.* 1. **侵權, 違規, 違反** — an act that disregards an agreement or a right
*“he claimed a violation of his rights under the Fifth Amendment”*
2. **輕罪, 違反** — a crime less serious than a felony

<a id="entry-i-001545"></a>

<a id="entry-i-001546"></a>
## infuse

**發音：** /ɪnˈfjuz/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **泡, 沏** — To steep in a liquid, so as to extract the soluble constituents (usually medicinal or herbal).
2. teach and impress by frequent repetitions or admonitions
*“inculcate values into the young generation”*
3. fill, as with a certain quality
*“The heavy traffic tinctures the air with carbon monoxide”*
4. undergo the process of infusion
*“the mint tea is infusing”*
5. let sit in a liquid to extract a flavor or to cleanse
*“steep the blossoms in oil”*
*“steep the fruit in alcohol”*
6. introduce into the body through a vein, for therapeutic purposes
*“Some physiologists infuses sugar solutions into the veins of animals”*

<a id="entry-i-001547"></a>
## infusion

*n.* 1. **析取液** — a solution obtained by steeping or soaking a substance (usually in water)
2. **泡製, 熬煮, 浸泡** — the process of extracting certain active properties (as a drug from a plant) by steeping or soaking (usually in water)
3. (medicine) the passive introduction of a substance (a fluid or drug or electrolyte) into a vein or between tissues (as by gravitational force)
4. the act of infusing or introducing a certain modifying element or quality
*“the team's continued success is attributable to a steady infusion of new talent”*

<a id="entry-i-001548"></a>
## ingathering

*n.* 1. **募捐, 募捐呼籲** — request for a sum of money
*“an appeal to raise money for starving children”*

<a id="entry-i-001549"></a>

2. **聰明的, 巧妙** — Of a thing, characterized by genius; cleverly contrived or done.
3. showing inventiveness and skill
*“a clever gadget”*
*“the cunning maneuvers leading to his success”*

<a id="entry-i-001550"></a>

*“a Hampshire farmer had fowls of different breeds, including Dorkings, and he discriminated ingeniously between the `dark ones' and the `white ones'”*

*“the cleverness of its design”*

*“the cleverness of its design”*

<a id="entry-i-001868"></a>
## instrumental

**發音：** /ɪnstɹəˈmɛntəl/
*adj.* 1. **為樂器譜曲+的, 樂器+的, 用樂器演奏+的** — relating to or designed for or performed on musical instruments
*“instrumental compositions”*
*“an instrumental ensemble”*
2. **有幫助的 /有幫助的, 可作為方法的 /可作為方法的, 可作為手段的 /可作為手段的** — Essential or central; of great importance or relevance.
3. serving or acting as a means or aid
*“instrumental in solving the crime”*
*n.* 1. **器樂 /器樂** — A composition written or performed without lyrics or singing, using a lead instrument to replace vocals.

<a id="entry-i-001869"></a>
## instrumental case

*n.* 1. **工具格, 具格** — A noun case used to express means or agency—and is generally indicated in English by by or with with the objective. Languages that use the instrumental case include Armenian, Serbo-Croatian, Czech, Dyirbal, Georgian, Latvian, Slovene, Polish, Ukrainian, Quechua, Russian, Assamese, Tamil, Turkish and Sanskrit. Extinct languages that used the instrumental case include Old English, Old Saxon and Old High German.

<a id="entry-i-001870"></a>
## instrumentalism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **工具主義 /工具主義** — In the philosophy of science, the view that concepts and theories are merely useful instruments whose worth is measured not by whether the concepts and theories are true or false (or correctly depict reality), but how effective they are in explaining and predicting phenomena.
2. a system of pragmatic philosophy that considers idea to be instruments that should guide our actions and their value is measured by their success

<a id="entry-i-001871"></a>
## instrumentality

*n.* 1. **儀器, 儀表** — an artifact (or system of artifacts) that is instrumental in accomplishing some end
2. a subsidiary organ of government created for a special purpose
*“are the judicial instrumentalities of local governments adequate?”*
*“he studied the French instrumentalities for law enforcement”*
3. the quality of being instrumental for some purpose

<a id="entry-i-001872"></a>

4. the act of arranging a piece of music for an orchestra and assigning parts to the different musical instruments

*“we are told that man is endowed with reason and capable of distinguishing good from evil”*
4. a person who uses the mind creatively

## intend

*v.* 1. *(ambitransitive, usually)* **打算, 想要, 意圖 /意圖** — To fix the mind upon (something, or something to be accomplished); be intent upon
2. **意味著, 意思是, 意指** — denote or connote
*“`maison' means `house' in French”*
*“An example sentence would show what this word means”*
3. have in mind as a purpose
*“I mean no harm”*
*“I only meant to help you”*

*“You never understand what I mean!”*

*“vivid green”*
*“intense blue”*

## intensify

*v.* 1. **強化, 逐步升級, 加強** — increase in extent or intensity
*“The Allies escalated the bombing”*
2. *(transitive)* **加劇 /加劇** — To render more intense.
3. make more intense, stronger, or more marked
*“The efforts were intensified", "Her rudeness intensified his dislike for her”*
*“Pot smokers claim it heightens their awareness”*
4. become more intense

<a id="entry-i-001995"></a>
## interchange

**發音：** /ˈɪntə(ɹ).tʃeɪndʒ/
*n.* 1. **交換, 兌換, 調換** — the act of changing one thing for another thing
*“Adam was promised immortality in exchange for his disobedience”*
*“there was an interchange of prisoners”*
2. *(countable)* **交換 /交換, 轉車 /轉車, 倒車 /倒車, 換乘站 /換乘站** — A connection between two or more lines, services or modes of transport; a station at which such a connection can be made.
3. a junction of highways on different levels that permits traffic to move from one to another without crossing traffic streams
4. mutual interaction; the activity of reciprocating or exchanging (especially information)
5. reciprocal transfer of equivalent sums of money (especially the currencies of different countries)
*“he earns his living from the interchange of currency”*
*v.* 1. **交替, 替, 替代, 代替, 替換** — put in the place of another; switch seemingly equivalent items
*“the con artist replaced the original with a fake Rembrandt”*
*“substitute regular milk with fat-free milk”*
2. **兌換, 更換, 調換, 交換** — give to, and receive from, one another
*“Would you change places with me?”*
*“We have been exchanging letters for a year”*
3. cause to change places
*“interchange this screw for one of a smaller size”*
4. reverse (a direction, attitude, or course of action)

<a id="entry-i-001996"></a>

2. **可互換+的, 可交換+的, 可交替+的, 可兌換+的, 可替換+的** — capable of replacing or changing places with something else; permitting mutual substitution without loss of function or suitability
*“interchangeable electric outlets" "interchangeable parts”*

<a id="entry-i-001997"></a>

<a id="entry-i-002020"></a>
## interest

**發音：** /ˈɪn.tɹɛst/
*n.* 1. **利息** — a fixed charge for borrowing money; usually a percentage of the amount borrowed
*“how much interest do you pay on your mortgage?”*
2. **權益, 股份, 利潤** — (law) a right or legal share of something; a financial involvement with something
*“they have interests all over the world”*
*“a stake in the company's future”*
3. **娛樂, 消遣** — a diversion that occupies one's time and thoughts (usually pleasantly)
*“sailing is her favorite pastime”*
*“his main pastime is gambling”*
4. *(uncountable, usually)* **利息** — The price paid for obtaining, or price received for providing, money or goods in a credit transaction, calculated as a fraction of the amount or value of what was borrowed.
5. *(countable, usually)* **利益** — An involvement, claim, right, share, stake in or link with a financial, business, or other undertaking or endeavor.
6. a sense of concern with and curiosity about someone or something
*“an interest in music”*
*v.* 1. **感興趣, 掛念, 關心** — be on the mind of
*“I worry about the second Germanic consonant shift”*
2. **要緊, 重要** — be of importance or consequence
*“This matters to me!”*
3. *(impersonal, obsolete, often)* **使……感興趣 /使……感興趣** — To be concerned with or engaged in; to affect; to concern; to excite.
4. excite the curiosity of; engage the interest of

<a id="entry-i-002021"></a>
## interest expense

*n.* 1. **利息支出, 利息費用** — interest paid on loans

<a id="entry-i-002022"></a>
## interest rate

*n.* 1. **利息率, 利率** — the percentage of a sum of money charged for its use
2. **利率, 利息率, 息率** — The percentage of an amount of money charged for its use per some period of time (often a year).

<a id="entry-i-002023"></a>
## interest-free

*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **無息 /無息** — Not charging interest; not subject to interest payments.

## interested

**發音：** /ˈɪnt.ə.ɹɪs.tɪd/
*adj.* 1. *(uncommon)* **持股** — Owning a share of a company; being a shareholder.
2. having or showing interest; especially curiosity or fascination or concern
*“an interested audience”*
*“interested in sports”*
3. involved in or affected by or having a claim to or share in
*“a memorandum to those concerned”*
*“an enterprise in which three men are concerned”*

2. **有趣, 有意思** — Arousing or holding the attention or interest of someone.

<a id="entry-i-002026"></a>
## interface

**發音：** /ˈɪntəfeɪs/
*n.* 1. **分界面** — (chemistry) a surface forming a common boundary between two things (two objects or liquids or chemical phases)
2. **用戶界面** — (computer science) a program that controls a display for the user (usually on a computer monitor) and that allows the user to interact with the system
3. **接口** — The point of interconnection between systems or subsystems.
4. **接口電路, 端口** — (computer science) computer circuit consisting of the hardware and associated circuitry that links one device with another (especially a computer and a hard disk drive or other peripherals)
5. **界面** — The connection between a user and a machine.
6. the overlap where two theories or phenomena affect each other or have links with each other
*“the interface between chemistry and biology”*

<a id="entry-i-002027"></a>
## interfacial

*adj.* 1. **界面+的** — relating to or situated at an interface
*“an interfacial layer”*
*“interfacial tension is the surface tension at the interface between two liquids”*

<a id="entry-i-002028"></a>
## interfere

**發音：** /ˌɪn.tɚˈfɪɚ/
*v.* 1. **干涉, 妨礙** — come between so as to be hindrance or obstacle
*“Your talking interferes with my work!”*
2. **介入, 調停, 干涉, 干預** — get involved, so as to alter or hinder an action, or through force or threat of force
*“Why did the U.S. not intervene earlier in WW II?”*
3. *(intransitive)* **妨礙 /妨礙, 阻礙 /阻礙, 幹擾, 插手, 幹預** — To get involved or involve oneself, causing disturbance.

*n.* 1. **干涉, 干預** — a policy of intervening in the affairs of other countries
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **阻礙 /阻礙, 干涉, 幹預** — The act of interfering with something, or something that interferes.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **干涉** — An effect caused by the superposition of two systems of waves.
4. **阻礙物, 障礙, 妨害, 妨礙** — any obstruction that impedes or is burdensome
5. the act of hindering or obstructing or impeding
6. electrical or acoustic activity that can disturb communication

2. **內部 /內部** — The inside of a building, container, cavern, or other enclosed structure.

*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **內部 /內部** — Within any limits, enclosure, or substance; inside; internal; inner.

*adv.* 1. **間歇+地** — in an intermittent manner
*“intermittently we questioned the barometer”*

<a id="entry-i-002081"></a>
## intermixture

*n.* 1. **摻入物, 混入物, 混合, 混合物** — an additional ingredient that is added by mixing with the base
*“the growing medium should be equal parts of sand and loam with an admixture of peat moss and cow manure”*
*“a large intermixture of sand”*
2. **混合** — the act of mixing together
*“paste made by a mix of flour and water”*
*“the mixing of sound channels in the recording studio”*
3. any foodstuff made by combining different ingredients
*“he volunteered to taste her latest concoction”*
*“he drank a mixture of beer and lemonade”*

<a id="entry-i-002082"></a>
## intermolecular

*adj.* 1. **分子間+的** — existing or acting between molecules
*“intermolecular forces”*
*“intermolecular condensation”*

<a id="entry-i-002083"></a>
## intern

**發音：** /ˈɪntɝn/
*n.* 1. **實習生 /實習生** — A student or recent graduate who works in order to gain experience in their chosen field.
2. an advanced student or graduate in medicine gaining supervised practical experience (`houseman' is a British term)
*v.* 1. **扣押, 拘留, 扣留** — deprive of freedom
*“During WW II, Japanese were interned in camps in the West”*
2. **實習** — work as an intern
*“The young doctor is interning at the Medical Center this year”*

<a id="entry-i-002084"></a>
## internal

**發音：** /ɪnˈtɜː.nəl/
*adj.* 1. **體內+的, 內部+的** — happening or arising or located within some limits or especially surface
*“internal organs”*
*“internal mechanism of a toy”*
2. **體內 /體內, 內 /內** — Within the body.
3. occurring within an institution or community
*“intragroup squabbling within the corporation”*
4. inside the country
*“the British Home Office has broader responsibilities than the United States Department of the Interior”*
*“the nation's internal politics”*
5. located inward
*“Beethoven's manuscript looks like a bloody record of a tremendous inner battle"- Leonard Bernstein”*
*“she thinks she has no soul, no interior life, but the truth is that she has no access to it"- David Denby”*
6. innermost or essential
*“the inner logic of Cubism”*
*“the internal contradictions of the theory”*

<a id="entry-i-002085"></a>
## internal angle

*n.* 1. **內角** — the angle inside two adjacent sides of a polygon

<a id="entry-i-002086"></a>
## internal auditory canal

*n.* 1. **內耳道 /內耳道, 內聽道 /內聽道** — A short auditory canal in the petrous portion of the temporal bone through which pass the facial and vestibulocochlear nerves and the labyrinthine artery.

<a id="entry-i-002087"></a>
## internal carotid artery

*n.* 1. **頸內動脈 /頸內動脈** — The inner branch of the carotid artery that supplies the brain, eyes, and other internal structures of the head.
2. the branch of the carotid artery that supplies blood to the brain and eyes and internal parts of the head

<a id="entry-i-002088"></a>
## internal carotid plexus

*n.* 1. **內頸動脈神經叢 /內頸動脈神經叢** — A network of nerves of the sympathetic nervous system surrounding the internal carotid artery.

<a id="entry-i-002089"></a>
## internal combustion

*n.* 1. **內燃** — the combustion of fuel inside a cylinder (as in an internal-combustion engine)
2. *(uncountable)* **內燃 /內燃** — The process where fuel is burned within an engine such as a diesel engine, producing power directly as opposed to externally such as in a steam engine.

<a id="entry-i-002090"></a>
## internal combustion engine

*n.* 1. **內燃機 /內燃機** — A piston or a rotary heat engine directly powered by the products of intermittent combustion of a fuel.

<a id="entry-i-002091"></a>
## internal condom

*n.* 1. **女性避孕套** — A device that is inserted into the vagina or anus during sexual intercourse as a barrier contraceptive to reduce the risk of sexually transmitted infections and unintended pregnancy.

<a id="entry-i-002092"></a>
## internal control

*n.* 1. **內控, 內部控制** — an accounting procedure or system designed to promote efficiency or assure the implementation of a policy or safeguard assets or avoid fraud and error etc.

<a id="entry-i-002093"></a>
## internal drive

*n.* 1. **內部驅動器** — a drive mounted inside of a computer

<a id="entry-i-002094"></a>
## internal jugular vein

*n.* 1. **頸內靜脈 /頸內靜脈** — A vein that collects the blood from the interior of the cranium, the superficial part of the face, and the neck, runs down the neck on the outside of the internal and common carotid arteries, and unites with the subclavian vein to form the brachiocephalic vein.
2. a continuation of the sigmoid sinus of the dura mater; joins the subclavian vein to form the brachiocephalic vein

<a id="entry-i-002095"></a>
## internal medicine

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **內科 /內科** — The medical specialty concerned with the diagnosis, management and nonsurgical treatment of diseases, especially of internal organ systems.
2. the branch of medicine that deals with the diagnosis and (nonsurgical) treatment of diseases of the internal organs (especially in adults)

<a id="entry-i-002096"></a>
## internal oblique muscle

*n.* 1. **腹內斜肌 /腹內斜肌** — A flat muscle situated under the external oblique muscle in the lateral and ventral part of the abdominal wall, that have aponeuroses extending medially to ensheathe the rectus muscles and fusing in the midventral line in the linea alba, and that act to compress the abdominal contents and to assist in expelling the contents of various visceral organs (as in urination, defecation, parturition, and expiration).

<a id="entry-i-002097"></a>
## internal occipital protuberance

*n.* 1. **枕內隆凸 /枕內隆凸** — A prominence on the inner surface of the occipital bone between the upper border and the foramen magnum.

<a id="entry-i-002098"></a>
## internal organ

*n.* 1. **內臟 /內臟** — An organ, such as the heart or colon, that is entirely within the confines of the body.
2. a main organ that is situated inside the body

<a id="entry-i-002099"></a>
## internal revenue

*n.* 1. **國內稅收** — government revenue from domestic sources (excluding customs)

<a id="entry-i-002100"></a>
## internal-combustion engine

*n.* 1. **內燃機** — a heat engine in which combustion occurs inside the engine rather than in a separate furnace; heat expands a gas that either moves a piston or turns a gas turbine

<a id="entry-i-002101"></a>
## internalize

**發音：** /ɪnˈtɝnəlaɪz/
*v.* 1. *(British, English, Oxford)* **使內在化 /使內在化** — To make something internal; to incorporate it in oneself.
2. incorporate within oneself; make subjective or personal
*“internalize a belief”*

<a id="entry-i-002102"></a>
## internally displaced person

**發音：** /ɪnˌtɜːnəli dɪsˌpleɪst ˈpɜːsn̩/
*n.* 1. **國內流離失所者 /國內流離失所者** — Someone who is forced to flee their home but who remains within their country's borders.

<a id="entry-i-002103"></a>
## international

**發音：** /ˌɪn.təˈnæʃ.(ə.)n(ə)l/
*adj.* 1. **國際+的** — concerning or belonging to all or at least two or more nations
*“international affairs”*
*“an international agreement”*
2. **國際 /國際** — Between or among nations.
3. from or between other countries
*“external commerce”*
*“international trade”*
*n.* 1. **萬國公黨 /萬國公黨, 國際政黨 /國際政黨** — A transnational organization of political parties of similar ideology.
2. any of several international socialist organizations

<a id="entry-i-002104"></a>
## international airport

*n.* 1. **國際機場 /國際機場** — An airport that accepts international flights.

<a id="entry-i-002105"></a>

*n.* 1. **安培** — a former unit of electric current (slightly smaller than the SI ampere)

<a id="entry-i-002106"></a>
## International Atomic Energy Agency

*prop. n.* 1. **國際原子能總署 /國際原子能總署** — Intergovernmental organization that seeks to promote the peaceful use of nuclear energy and to inhibit its use for any military purpose.

<a id="entry-i-002107"></a>
## international auxiliary language

*n.* 1. **國際輔助語 /國際輔助語** — A language intended to be used by people who do not share a common language.

<a id="entry-i-002108"></a>
## international candle

*n.* 1. **國際燭光** — a former international unit of luminous intensity; now replaced by the candela

<a id="entry-i-002109"></a>
## International Court of Justice

*prop. n.* 1. **國際法院 /國際法院** — A judicial body of the United Nations, established in 1945 to decide disputes brought by nations.

<a id="entry-i-002110"></a>
## International Date Line

*prop. n.* 1. **國際日期變更線 /國際日期變更線** — An imaginary line on the Earth's surface at 180° longitude, mostly through the Pacific Ocean, to the east of which the calendar date is one day earlier than to the west.

<a id="entry-i-002111"></a>
## International Day of the Girl Child

*prop. n.* 1. **國際女童日 /國際女童日** — A holiday celebrated on October 11 that aims to raise awareness of the challenges and issues faced by girls around the world

<a id="entry-i-002112"></a>
## International Klein Blue

*prop. n.* 1. **國際奇連藍 /國際奇連藍, 國際克萊因藍 /國際克萊因藍** — An intense deep blue hue achieved by means of ultramarine pigment and a binder.

<a id="entry-i-002113"></a>
## international law

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **國際法 /國際法, 萬國公法 /萬國公法, 萬國法 /萬國法, 國際公法 /國際公法** — Set of rules generally regarded and accepted as binding in relations between states and between nations, serving as a framework for the practice of stable and organized international relations.
2. the body of laws governing relations between nations

<a id="entry-i-002114"></a>
## international mile

*n.* 1. **英里** — a unit of length equal to 1,760 yards or 5,280 feet; exactly 1609.344 meters

<a id="entry-i-002115"></a>
## International Monetary Fund

*prop. n.* 1. **國際貨幣基金組織 /國際貨幣基金組織** — The international organization entrusted with overseeing the global financial system by monitoring foreign exchange rates and balance of payments, as well as offering technical and financial assistance when requested.

<a id="entry-i-002116"></a>
## international nautical mile

*n.* 1. **航海測鏈, 海里** — a unit of length used in navigation; exactly 1,852 meters; historically based on the distance spanned by one minute of arc in latitude

<a id="entry-i-002117"></a>
## international organization

2. an international alliance involving many different countries

<a id="entry-i-002118"></a>
## International Phonetic Alphabet

**發音：** /ˌɪntəˈnæʃ(ə)nəl fəˈnɛtɪk ˈælfəˌbɛt/
*prop. n.* 1. **國際音標 /國際音標** — A standardized set of symbols for representing the sounds of human speech.

<a id="entry-i-002119"></a>
## international relations

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **國際關係 /國際關係** — The study of political and trade relationships among countries.

<a id="entry-i-002120"></a>
## international scale

<a id="entry-i-002121"></a>
## international scientific vocabulary

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **國際科技詞彙** — Words used in various languages with similar meanings and pronunciations as a consequence of internationalizing scientific progress.

<a id="entry-i-002122"></a>
## International Space Station

*prop. n.* 1. **國際空間站 /國際空間站, 國際太空站 /國際太空站** — An artificial satellite in low Earth orbit operated by a number of cooperating space programs.

<a id="entry-i-002123"></a>
## international student

*n.* 1. **國際學生 /國際學生, 留學生 /留學生, 留子, 國際生 /國際生** — A student who studies in an institution of higher learning in a foreign country and is required to hold a student visa.

<a id="entry-i-002124"></a>
## international system

*n.* 1. **國際單位制** — a complete metric system of units of measurement for scientists; fundamental quantities are length (meter) and mass (kilogram) and time (second) and electric current (ampere) and temperature (kelvin) and amount of matter (mole) and luminous intensity (candela)
*“Today the United States is the only country in the world not totally committed to the Systeme International d'Unites”*

<a id="entry-i-002125"></a>
## International System of Units

*prop. n.* 1. **國際單位制 /國際單位制, 萬國公制 /萬國公制** — A standard set of units of measurement for use in scientific literature worldwide. The newest and most widespread version of the metric system.

<a id="entry-i-002126"></a>
## international system of units

*n.* 1. **國際單位制** — a complete metric system of units of measurement for scientists; fundamental quantities are length (meter) and mass (kilogram) and time (second) and electric current (ampere) and temperature (kelvin) and amount of matter (mole) and luminous intensity (candela)
*“Today the United States is the only country in the world not totally committed to the Systeme International d'Unites”*

<a id="entry-i-002127"></a>
## international unit

*n.* 1. **國際單位 /國際單位** — A particular, minute unit of mass, defined differently for different substances, but so that varying substances of the same general type have the property that one international unit of the one has the same effect on the human body as one international unit of the other.

<a id="entry-i-002128"></a>
## international waters

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **公海** — Those parts of the ocean that are not legally recognized as being under the jurisdiction of any national government.
2. the open seas of the world outside the territorial waters of any nation

<a id="entry-i-002129"></a>
## International Women's Day

*prop. n.* 1. **國際婦女節 /國際婦女節** — A holiday celebrated on March 8, with a focus on women's liberation, rights, class struggle, and femininity in general.

## Internationale

**發音：** /ˌɪn.tɚˌnæ.ʃənˈæl/
*prop. n.* 1. **國際歌 /國際歌, 英特納雄耐爾** — A general left-wing anthem for solidarity and emancipation of the working class, dating back to the 19th century.

<a id="entry-i-002131"></a>
## internationalise

*v.* 1. **使國際化** — put under international control
*“internationalize trade of certain drugs”*
2. **置於國際管理下, 使國際化** — make international in character
*“We internationalized the committee”*

<a id="entry-i-002132"></a>
## internationalism

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **國際主義 /國際主義** — Political, economic and cultural cooperation between nations.
2. the doctrine that nations should cooperate because their common interests are more important than their differences
3. quality of being international in scope
*“he applauded the internationality of scientific terminology”*

<a id="entry-i-002133"></a>
## internationalization

**發音：** /ɪntəˌnæʃ(ə)n(ə)ləˈzeɪʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **國際化 /國際化** — The conversion of something in order to make it international.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **國際化 /國際化** — The act or process of making a product suitable for international markets, typically by making text messages easily translatable and ensuring support of non-Latin character sets, languages, and regions.
3. the act of bringing something under international control

<a id="entry-i-002134"></a>
## internationalize

*v.* 1. *(ambitransitive)* **國際化 /國際化** — To make something international; to involve multiple nations in.
2. **使國際化** — put under international control
*“internationalize trade of certain drugs”*
3. **置於國際管理下, 使國際化** — make international in character
*“We internationalized the committee”*

<a id="entry-i-002135"></a>
## internecine

**發音：** /ˌɪntəˈniːsaɪn/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **自相殘殺的 /自相殘殺的, 兩敗俱傷的 /兩敗俱傷的** — Mutually destructive; most often applied to warfare.
2. *(not-comparable)* **內部（衝突）的 /內部（衝突）的** — Characterized by struggle within a group, usually applied to an ethnic or familial relationship.
3. (of conflict) within a group or organization
*“an internecine feud among proxy holders”*
4. characterized by bloodshed and carnage for both sides
*“internecine war”*

*“the internees were enemy aliens and suspected terrorists”*

*prop. n.* 1. **互聯網 /互聯網, 網絡 /網絡, 網路 /網路, 因特網 /因特網, 網際網路 /網際網路** — The global, decentralized network of computers which communicate with one another using the Internet protocol suite, which now forms a vital part of global communication, content sharing, and information exchange systems.

<a id="entry-i-002138"></a>
## internet

<a id="entry-i-002140"></a>
## Internet cafe

*n.* 1. **網吧 /網吧, 互聯網咖啡館 /互聯網咖啡館, 因特網咖啡廳 /因特網咖啡廳, 網咖 /網咖, 網絡咖啡屋 /網絡咖啡屋, 上網咖啡館 /上網咖啡館** — A place where one can use a computer with Internet access for a fee.

<a id="entry-i-002142"></a>

3. the action of interjecting or interposing an action or remark that interrupts

<a id="entry-i-002155"></a>
## interpersonal

*adj.* 1. **人際+的** — occurring among or involving several people
*“interpersonal situations in which speech occurs”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **人際的 /人際的, 人與人之間的 /人與人之間的** — Between two or more people.

<a id="entry-i-002156"></a>
## interpersonal chemistry

*“a mysterious alchemy brought them together”*

## interplanetary

2. **行星間+的, 行星際+的** — between or among planets
*“interplanetary travel”*

<a id="entry-i-002159"></a>
## Interpol

**發音：** /ˌʔin.teɾˈpol/
*prop. n.* 1. **國際刑警組織 /國際刑警組織** — The International Criminal Police Organization, an international organization facilitating police cooperation.

<a id="entry-i-002160"></a>
## interpolation

**發音：** /ɪnˌtɜː.pəˈleɪʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **插值** — The process of estimating the value of a function at a point from its values at nearby points.
2. a message (spoken or written) that is introduced or inserted
*“with the help of his friend's interpolations his story was eventually told”*
*“with many insertions in the margins”*
3. (mathematics) calculation of the value of a function between the values already known
4. the action of interjecting or interposing an action or remark that interrupts

<a id="entry-i-002161"></a>
## interpose

*v.* 1. **放於, 插入** — be or come between
*“An interposing thicket blocked their way”*
2. **插手, 干預** — introduce
*“God interposed death”*
3. **介入, 調停, 干涉, 干預** — get involved, so as to alter or hinder an action, or through force or threat of force
*“Why did the U.S. not intervene earlier in WW II?”*
4. to insert between other elements
*“She interjected clever remarks”*

<a id="entry-i-002162"></a>
## interpret

**發音：** /ɪnˈtɜː.pɹɪt/
*v.* 1. **口譯, 翻譯, 轉譯** — restate (words) from one language into another language
*“I have to translate when my in-laws from Austria visit the U.S.”*
*“Can you interpret the speech of the visiting dignitaries?”*
2. *(intransitive)* **翻譯 /翻譯, 傳譯 /傳譯** — To convey what a user of one language is saying or signing, in real time or shortly after that person has finished communicating, to a user of a different language.
3. make sense of; assign a meaning to
*“What message do you see in this letter?”*
*“How do you interpret his behavior?”*
4. give an interpretation or explanation to
5. give an interpretation or rendition of
*“The pianist rendered the Beethoven sonata beautifully”*
6. create an image or likeness of
*“The painter represented his wife as a young girl”*

<a id="entry-i-002163"></a>
## interpretation

**發音：** /ɪnˌtɜːpɹəˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable)* **解釋 /解釋** — A sense given by an interpreter; an exposition or explanation given; meaning.
2. *(uncountable)* **口譯 /口譯** — The discipline or study of translating one spoken or signed language into another (as opposed to translation, which concerns itself with written language).
3. a mental representation of the meaning or significance of something
4. the act of interpreting something as expressed in an artistic performance
*“her rendition of Milton's verse was extraordinarily moving”*
5. an explanation that results from interpreting something
*“the report included his interpretation of the forensic evidence”*
6. an explanation of something that is not immediately obvious
*“the edict was subject to many interpretations”*
*“he annoyed us with his interpreting of parables”*

<a id="entry-i-002164"></a>
## interpreter

**發音：** /ɪnˈtɜː.pɹɪ.tə/
*n.* 1. **代言人, 解釋者** — an advocate who represents someone else's policy or purpose
*“the meeting was attended by spokespersons for all the major organs of government”*
2. **口譯員 /口譯員, 口譯者 /口譯者, 譯員 /譯員, 翻譯 /翻譯, 翻譯員 /翻譯員, 翻譯者 /翻譯者, 通事, 通譯 /通譯, 傳譯員 /傳譯員, 舌人, 解釋程序 /解釋程序** — A program that converts commands into machine language in real time rather than compiling it in advance.
3. someone who mediates between speakers of different languages
4. someone who uses art to represent something
*“his paintings reveal a sensitive interpreter of nature”*
*“she was famous as an interpreter of Shakespearean roles”*
5. (computer science) a program that translates and executes source language statements one line at a time

<a id="entry-i-002165"></a>
## interpunct

*n.* 1. **間隔號** — The punctuation mark ⟨·⟩.

2. between races

3. involving or composed of different races
*“interracial schools”*
*“a mixed neighborhood”*

<a id="entry-i-002167"></a>

*“I cannot interrelate these two events”*

2. **互聯的, 互相關聯的** — Having a mutual or reciprocal relation or parallelism; correlative.

*n.* 1. **相互練習, 相互關係** — mutual or reciprocal relation or relatedness
*“interrelationships of animal structure and function”*

**發音：** /ˌɪn.tɚ.ɹɪˈleɪ.ʃən/

*“interrelationships of animal structure and function”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **相互關係 /相互關係** — Mutual or reciprocal relation.

<a id="entry-i-002172"></a>
## interrelationship

*n.* 1. **相互練習, 相互關係** — mutual or reciprocal relation or relatedness
*“interrelationships of animal structure and function”*

<a id="entry-i-002173"></a>
## interrobang

**發音：** /ɪnˈtɛ.ɹəʊˌbæŋ/
*n.* 1. **疑問驚嘆號 /疑問驚歎號** — The nonstandard punctuation mark ⟨‽⟩ (a combination of ⟨?⟩ and ⟨!⟩), which may be used at the end of a sentence to express excitement or disbelief, or to indicate that it is a rhetorical question.

<a id="entry-i-002174"></a>
## interrogate

**發音：** /ɪnˈtɛɹəɡeɪt/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **審問 /審問, 審訊 /審訊** — To question or quiz, especially in a thorough or aggressive manner.
2. **審問, 詢問** — pose a series of questions to
*“The suspect was questioned by the police”*
*“We questioned the survivor about the details of the explosion”*
3. transmit (a signal) for setting off an appropriate response, as in telecommunication

<a id="entry-i-002175"></a>
## interrogation

**發音：** /ɪnˌteɹ.əˈɡeɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **疑問 /疑問** — The act of interrogating or questioning; an examination by questions; an inquiry.
2. **審問, 探問, 詢問, 問題, 質問** — an instance of questioning
*“there was a question about my training”*
*“we made inquiries of all those who were present”*
3. a sentence of inquiry that asks for a reply
*“he asked a direct question”*
*“he had trouble phrasing his interrogations”*
4. a transmission that will trigger an answering transmission from a transponder
5. formal systematic questioning

<a id="entry-i-002176"></a>
## interrogative

*n.* 1. **疑問語氣** — some linguists consider interrogative sentences to constitute a mood
2. a sentence of inquiry that asks for a reply
*“he asked a direct question”*
*“he had trouble phrasing his interrogations”*
*adj.* 1. **疑問+的** — relating to verbs in the so-called interrogative mood
*“not all questions have an interrogative construction”*
2. relating to the use of or having the nature of an interrogation

<a id="entry-i-002177"></a>
## interrogative mood

*n.* 1. **疑問語氣** — some linguists consider interrogative sentences to constitute a mood

<a id="entry-i-002178"></a>
## interrogative pronoun

*n.* 1. **疑問代詞 /疑問代詞** — A pronoun used in a question. In English, the five interrogative pronouns are what, which, who, whom, and whose (also used as relative pronouns). These require no antecedent.

<a id="entry-i-002179"></a>
## interrogatively

*adv.* 1. **疑問+地** — in a questioning format
2. with curiosity
*“the baby looked around curiously”*

<a id="entry-i-002180"></a>
## interrupt

**發音：** /ˌɪntəˈɹʌpt/
*v.* 1. **切斷, 打斷, 中斷, 中止** — make a break in
*“We interrupt the program for the following messages”*
2. **干擾, 插嘴, 中斷, 打斷** — interfere in someone else's activity
*“Please don't interrupt me while I'm on the phone”*
3. *(ambitransitive)* **打斷 /打斷, 插嘴, 中斷 /中斷, 打擾 /打擾** — To disturb or halt (an ongoing process or action, or the person performing it) by interfering suddenly, especially by speaking.
4. **使中止, 暫停, 結束, 終止, 中止** — terminate
*“She interrupted her pregnancy”*
*“break a lucky streak”*
5. destroy the peace or tranquility of
*“Don't interrupt me when I'm reading”*
*n.* 1. a signal that temporarily stops the execution of a program so that another procedure can be carried out

<a id="entry-i-002181"></a>

*n.* 1. **斷續器** — a device for automatically interrupting an electric current

<a id="entry-i-002182"></a>

*n.* 1. **打斷, 中斷, 中止** — an act of delaying or interrupting the continuity
*“it was presented without commercial breaks”*
*“there was a gap in his account”*

3. **暫停, 暫時的停頓, 中斷, 中止, 間歇** — a time interval during which there is a temporary cessation of something
4. some abrupt occurrence that interrupts an ongoing activity
*“the telephone is an annoying interruption”*
*“there was a break in the action when a player was hurt”*

<a id="entry-i-002184"></a>

4. **交叉點** — a point or set of points common to two or more geometric configurations

3. **間隔 /間隔, 間隙 /間隙** — A period of time.

<a id="entry-i-002205"></a>

**發音：** /ˌɪntəˈviːn/

*“Why did the U.S. not intervene earlier in WW II?”*
2. **介於中間, 發生於其間, 介入, 插入** — be placed or located between other things or extend between spaces and events
*“This interludes intervenes between the two movements”*

*“the war intervened between the birth of her two children”*

<a id="entry-i-002206"></a>
## intervention

**發音：** /ɪntɚˈvɛnʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **介入, 幹預, 干涉** — The action of intervening; interfering in some course of events.

*“it occurs without human intervention”*
5. the act or fact of interposing one thing between or among others
6. (law) a proceeding that permits a person to enter into a lawsuit already in progress; admission of person not an original party to the suit so that person can protect some right or interest that is allegedly affected by the proceedings
*“the purpose of intervention is to prevent unnecessary duplication of lawsuits”*

*“my interviews with teenagers revealed a weakening of religious bonds”*

## intuition

**發音：** /ˌɪn.tjuːˈɪʃ.ən/
*n.* 1. **嫌疑, 直覺, 懷疑** — an impression that something might be the case
*“he had an intuition that something had gone wrong”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **直覺 /直覺, 直感** — A perceptive insight gained by the use of this faculty.
3. instinctive knowing (without the use of rational processes)

<a id="entry-i-002309"></a>
## intuitionism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **直覺主義 /直覺主義, 直觀主義 /直觀主義** — An approach to mathematics/logic which avoids proof by contradiction, and which requires that, in order to prove that something exists, one must construct it.
2. (philosophy) the doctrine that knowledge is acquired primarily by intuition

<a id="entry-i-002310"></a>
## intuitionist

*adj.* 1. **直觀主義+的, 直覺主義+的, 直覺主義者+的** — of or relating to intuitionism

<a id="entry-i-002311"></a>
## intuitive

**發音：** /ɪnˈtjuːɪtɪv/
*adj.* 1. **直覺的 /直覺的** — Spontaneous, without requiring conscious thought.
2. **直覺+的** — spontaneously derived from or prompted by a natural tendency
*“an intuitive revulsion”*
3. obtained through intuition rather than from reasoning or observation

<a id="entry-i-002312"></a>
## intuitive feeling

*n.* 1. **知覺** — an intuitive understanding of something
*“he had a great feeling for music”*

<a id="entry-i-002313"></a>
## intuitively

*adv.* 1. **直觀+地, 直覺+地** — in an intuitive manner
*“inventors seem to have chosen intuitively a combination of explosive and aggressive sounds as warning signals to be used on automobiles”*

<a id="entry-i-002314"></a>
## intumesce

*v.* 1. **像泡一樣冒起, 噗噗地往上冒, 往上冒泡** — move upwards in bubbles, as from the effect of heating; also used metaphorically
*“Gases bubbled up from the earth”*
*“Marx's ideas have bubbled up in many places in Latin America”*
2. **腫大, 膨脹, 擴大, 隆起** — expand abnormally
*“The bellies of the starving children are swelling”*

<a id="entry-i-002315"></a>
## intumescence

*n.* 1. **腫塊** — swelling up with blood or other fluids (as with congestion)
2. **膨脹, 增大** — the increase in volume of certain substances when they are heated (often accompanied by release of water)

<a id="entry-i-002316"></a>

*n.* 1. **腫塊** — swelling up with blood or other fluids (as with congestion)
2. **膨脹, 增大** — the increase in volume of certain substances when they are heated (often accompanied by release of water)

*“the invagination of the blastula”*
2. **內填** — (biology) growth in the surface area of a cell by the deposit of new particles between existing particles in the cell wall

*“The images flooded his mind”*
2. **氾濫 /氾濫, 氾濫** — To overwhelm.
3. **淹沒 /淹沒** — To cover with large amounts of water; to flood.
4. fill or cover completely, usually with water

*“plains fertilized by annual inundations”*

## invade

**發音：** /ɪnˈveɪd/
*v.* 1. **侵入, 侵犯** — to intrude upon, infringe, encroach on, violate
*“This new colleague invades my territory”*
*“The neighbors intrude on your privacy”*
2. *(transitive)* **入侵, 侵入, 侵略, 侵犯, 侵佔, 侵攻** — To enter by force, usually in order to conquer.
3. **大批出沒, 大批滋生, 氾濫** — occupy in large numbers or live on a host
*“the Kudzu plant infests much of the South and is spreading to the North”*
4. march aggressively into another's territory by military force for the purposes of conquest and occupation
*“Hitler invaded Poland on September 1, 1939”*
5. penetrate or assault, in a harmful or injurious way
*“The cancer had invaded her lungs”*

## inverse

**發音：** /ɪnˈvɝs/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **逆, 逆向** — Opposite in effect, nature or order.
2. *(not-comparable)* **逆, 逆向** — Reverse, opposite in order.
3. *(not-comparable)* **逆** — Having the properties of an inverse; said with reference to any two operations, which, when both are performed in succession upon any quantity, reproduce that quantity.
4. reversed (turned backward) in order or nature or effect
5. opposite in nature or effect or relation to another quantity
*“a term is in inverse proportion to another term if it increases (or decreases) as the other decreases (or increases)”*

*“when the direct approach failed he tried the inverse”*

**發音：** /ɪnˈvɚ.ʒən/
*n.* 1. **倒位, 內向, 內翻** — abnormal condition in which an organ is turned inward or inside out (as when the upper part of the uterus is pulled into the cervical canal after childbirth)

3. **詞語倒裝法** — the reversal of the normal order of words
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **倒裝 /倒裝** — Deviation from standard word order, as for example by putting the predicate before the subject. It takes place in questions with auxiliary verbs; in normal, affirmative clauses beginning with a negative particle, for the purpose of emphasis; and in other rhetorical devices or unusual situations.
5. the layer of air near the earth is cooler than an overlying layer
6. (genetics) a kind of mutation in which the order of the genes in a section of a chromosome is reversed

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **無敵 /無敵, 不敗 /不敗** — The quality or state of being invincible; invincibleness.
2. the property being difficult or impossible to defeat

<a id="entry-i-002377"></a>
## invincible

**發音：** /ɪnˈvɪn.sə.bəl/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **無敵 /無敵, 不敗 /不敗** — Impossible to defeat, destroy, or kill; too powerful to be defeated or overcome.
2. incapable of being overcome or subdued
*“an invincible army”*
*“her invincible spirit”*

<a id="entry-i-002378"></a>

<a id="entry-i-002379"></a>
## inviolable

*adj.* 1. **不可違反 /不可違反** — Not violable; not to be infringed.
2. **不可侵犯+的, 不可違背+的, 不可褻瀆+的, 神聖+的** — incapable of being transgressed or dishonored
*“the person of the king is inviolable”*
*“an inviolable oath”*
3. **不可戰勝+的, 不能征服+的, 堅不可摧+的, 攻不可破+的, 無法攻取+的** — immune to attack; incapable of being tampered with
*“an impregnable fortress”*
*“fortifications that made the frontier inviolable”*
4. **不可侵犯** — Incapable of being injured or invaded; indestructible.
5. **不可褻瀆+的, 極神聖+的, 神聖不可侵犯+的, 不可侵犯+的, 神聖+的** — must be kept sacred
6. not capable of being violated or infringed
*“infrangible human rights”*

<a id="entry-i-002380"></a>

2. (of a woman) having the hymen unbroken
*“she was intact, virginal”*

*“invisible rays”*

## invitation

**發音：** /ɪnvɪˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **邀請, 邀請函** — a request (spoken or written) to participate or be present or take part in something
*“an invitation to lunch”*
*“she threw the invitation away”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **邀請 /邀請** — The act of inviting; solicitation; the requesting of a person's company.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **邀請函 /邀請函, 請柬 /請柬, 請帖 /請帖** — A document or verbal message conveying an invitation.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **吸引, 引誘 /引誘** — Allurement; enticement.
5. a tempting allurement
*“she was an invitation to trouble”*

<a id="entry-i-002387"></a>

<a id="entry-i-002388"></a>
## invite

**發音：** /ɪnˈvaɪt/
*v.* 1. **約請, 請, 邀請** — invite someone to one's house
*“Can I invite you for dinner on Sunday night?”*
2. **請, 邀請** — ask someone in a friendly way to do something
3. **招待, 邀請** — have as a guest
*“I invited them to a restaurant”*
4. *(transitive)* **邀請 /邀請, 請 /請, 約請 /約請** — To ask for the presence or participation of someone or something.
5. increase the likelihood of
*“ask for trouble”*
*“invite criticism”*
6. give rise to a desire by being attractive or inviting
*“the window displays tempted the shoppers”*
*n.* 1. a colloquial expression for invitation
*“he didn't get no invite to the party”*

<a id="entry-i-002389"></a>

*“Has he asked you out yet?”*

<a id="entry-i-002390"></a>
## invitee

<a id="entry-i-002391"></a>
## invoice

**發音：** /ˈɪnˌvɔɪs/
*n.* 1. **發票 /發票, 發單 /發單, 收費明細 /收費明細, 費用清單 /費用清單, 收據 /收據** — A bill; a commercial document issued by a seller to a buyer indicating the products, quantities and agreed prices for products or services that the seller has already provided the buyer with. An invoice indicates that, unless paid in advance, payment is due by the buyer to the seller, according to the agreed terms.
2. an itemized statement of money owed for goods shipped or services rendered
*“he paid his bill and left”*
*“send me an account of what I owe”*
*v.* 1. **開發票** — send an bill to
*“She invoiced the company for her expenses”*

<a id="entry-i-002392"></a>
## invoke

**發音：** /ɪnˈvoʊk/
*v.* 1. **求助** — request earnestly (something from somebody); ask for aid or protection
*“appeal to somebody for help”*
*“Invoke God in times of trouble”*
2. *(transitive)* **調動 /調動** — To cause (a program or subroutine) to execute.
3. summon into action or bring into existence, often as if by magic
*“raise the specter of unemployment”*
*“he conjured wild birds in the air”*
4. cite as an authority; resort to
*“He invoked the law that would save him”*
*“I appealed to the law of 1900”*

*“involuntary servitude”*

## involution

3. a long and intricate and complicated grammatical construction

*“the teacher tried to increase his students' engagement in class activities”*
6. the process of raising a quantity to some assigned power

<a id="entry-i-002401"></a>
## involve

*v.* 1. **關係, 使捲入, 使陷入, 影響, 涉及** — connect closely and often incriminatingly
*“This new ruling affects your business”*
2. **使捲入, 使牽涉, 使陷入** — engage as a participant
*“Don't involve me in your family affairs!”*
3. **使必要, 包含有, 伴隨** — have as a necessary feature
*“This decision involves many changes”*
4. **要求, 必要, 要, 需, 需求** — require as useful, just, or proper
*“It takes nerve to do what she did”*
*“success usually requires hard work”*
5. **含有, 包括** — contain as a part
*“Dinner at Joe's always involves at least six courses”*
6. **使複雜** — make complex or intricate or complicated
*“The situation was rather involved”*

<a id="entry-i-002402"></a>
## involved

*adj.* 1. **複雜難懂+的, 費解+的, 複雜+的, 錯綜複雜+的** — highly complex or intricate and occasionally devious
*“the Byzantine tax structure”*
*“Byzantine methods for holding on to his chairmanship”*
2. connected by participation or association or use
*“we accomplished nothing, simply because of the large number of people involved”*
*“the problems involved”*
3. entangled or hindered as if e.g. in mire
*“the difficulties in which the question is involved”*
*“brilliant leadership mired in details and confusion”*
4. emotionally involved
5. enveloped
*“a castle involved in mist”*
*“the difficulties in which the question is involved”*

<a id="entry-i-002403"></a>
## involvement

*n.* 1. **捲入, 連累** — a connection of inclusion or containment
*“he escaped involvement in the accident”*
*“there was additional involvement of the liver and spleen”*
2. **偷情, 婚外情, 私通** — a usually secretive or illicit sexual relationship
3. **參與, 參加, 分享** — the condition of sharing in common with others (as fellows or partners etc.)
4. the act of sharing in the activities of a group
*“the teacher tried to increase his students' engagement in class activities”*
5. a sense of concern with and curiosity about someone or something
*“an interest in music”*

## irony

*n.* 1. **反話, 反語, 諷刺, 諷刺語言** — witty language used to convey insults or scorn

2. *(countable, rhetoric, uncountable)* **反語 /反語, 反話 /反話, 諷刺 /諷刺** — The quality of a statement that, when taken in context, may actually mean something different from, or the opposite of, what is written literally; the use of words expressing something other than their literal intention, often in a humorous context.

<a id="entry-i-002626"></a>

*phr.* 1. **要下雨了嗎？ /要下雨了嗎？** — Is it going to rain?

<a id="entry-i-002627"></a>
## is it safe here

*phr.* 1. **這裡安全嗎？ /這裡安全嗎？** — is it safe here?

## is that so

<a id="entry-i-002629"></a>
## is that the time

*phr.* 1. **都這麼晚了 /都這麼晚了** — Used to indicate surprise at how late in time it is.

<a id="entry-i-002630"></a>
## is there a grocery store nearby

<a id="entry-i-002631"></a>

<a id="entry-i-002632"></a>
## is there a smaller size

*phr.* 1. **有小一號的嗎？ /有小一號的嗎？** — Indicates that the speaker needs a smaller size (of clothes or shoes).

*“al-Gama'a al-Islamiyya uses force to push Egyptian society toward Islamic rule”*

*“the original Jihad was responsible for the assassination of Anwar Sadat in 1981”*
2. a Shiite terrorist organization with strong ties to Iran; seeks to create an Iranian fundamentalist Islamic state in Lebanon; car bombs are the signature weapon

4. place or set apart
*“They isolated the political prisoners from the other inmates”*
5. obtain in pure form
*“The chemist managed to isolate the compound”*
6. set apart from others
*“The dentist sequesters the tooth he is working on”*

<a id="entry-i-002727"></a>
## isolated

*adj.* 1. **孤立+的, 分離+的** — being or feeling set or kept apart from others
*“she felt detached from the group”*
*“could not remain the isolated figure he had been"- Sherwood Anderson”*
2. **孤立+的, 分離+的** — marked by separation of or from usually contiguous elements
*“little isolated worlds, as abruptly disjunct and unexpected as a palm-shaded well in the Sahara"- Scientific Monthly”*
3. **進退兩難+的, 孤立+的** — cut off or left behind
*“an isolated pawn”*
*“several stranded fish in a tide pool”*
4. **孤立+的, 分開+的** — remote and separate physically or socially
*“existed over the centuries as a world apart”*
*“preserved because they inhabited a place apart"- W.H.Hudson”*
5. not close together in time
*“isolated instances of rebellion”*
*“a few stray crumbs”*
6. under forced isolation especially for health reasons
*“a quarantined animal”*
*“isolated patients”*

<a id="entry-i-002728"></a>
## isolation

**發音：** /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **孤立, 隔離** — a state of separation between persons or groups
2. *(uncountable)* **隔離 /隔離, 隔斷 /隔斷** — The state of being isolated, detached, or separated; the state of being away from other people.
3. **隔離** — (psychiatry) a defense mechanism in which memory of an unacceptable act or impulse is separated from the emotion originally associated with it
4. a feeling of being disliked and alone
5. the act of isolating something; setting something apart from others
6. a country's withdrawal from international politics
*“he opposed a policy of American isolation”*

<a id="entry-i-002729"></a>
## isolationism

**發音：** /ˌaɪsəˈleɪʃəˌnɪzəm/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **孤立主義 /孤立主義** — A national (or group) policy of non-interaction with other nations (or groups).
2. a policy of nonparticipation in international economic and political relations

<a id="entry-i-002730"></a>
## isolationist

*adj.* 1. **孤立主義+的** — of or relating to isolationism
*n.* 1. an advocate of isolationism in international affairs

<a id="entry-i-002731"></a>
## isolationistic

*adj.* 1. **孤立主義+的** — of or relating to isolationism

<a id="entry-i-002733"></a>

2. **同核異能素 /同核異能素** — Any of two or more atomic nuclei with the same mass number and atomic number but with different radioactive properties.
3. a compound that exists in forms having different arrangements of atoms but the same molecular weight

2. an enzyme that catalyzes its substrate to an isomeric form

*adj.* 1. **同大+的, 同容積+的, 等體積+的, 等容積+的, 等尺寸+的** — related by an isometry

4. of a crystal system characterized by three equal axes at right angles

## isometry

*“the isometries of the cube”*

4. equality of measure (e.g., equality of height above sea level or equality of loudness etc.)

<a id="entry-i-002776"></a>
## issue

**發音：** /ˈɪʃuː/
*n.* 1. **爭議中的事, 待解決的事** — an important question that is in dispute and must be settled
*“the issue could be settled by requiring public education for everyone”*
*“politicians never discuss the real issues”*
2. *(obsolete)* **問題 /問題** — A movement of soldiers towards an enemy, a sortie.
3. **收益, 贏利, 利潤, 收入** — the income or profit arising from such transactions as the sale of land or other property
*“the average return was about 5%”*
4. **後果, 效果, 結果, 影響** — a phenomenon that follows and is caused by some previous phenomenon
*“the magnetic effect was greater when the rod was lengthwise”*
*“his decision had depressing consequences for business”*
5. **後嗣, 後裔, 子孫, 後代** — the immediate descendants of a person
*“she was the mother of many offspring”*
*“he died without issue”*
6. **出路, 出口** — an opening that permits escape or release
*“he blocked the way out”*
*“the canyon had only one issue”*
*v.* 1. **出版, 發佈, 發表, 發行** — prepare and issue for public distribution or sale
*“publish a magazine or newspaper”*
2. **分發, 分配** — circulate or distribute or equip with
*“issue a new uniform to the children”*
*“supply blankets for the beds”*
3. bring out an official document (such as a warrant)
4. come out of
*“Water issued from the hole in the wall”*
*“The words seemed to come out by themselves”*
5. make out and issue
*“write out a check”*
*“cut a ticket”*

<a id="entry-i-002777"></a>
## issue forth

*v.* 1. **出來, 出現** — come forth
*“A scream came from the woman's mouth”*
*“His breath came hard”*

## itch

**發音：** /ɪt͡ʃ/
*v.* 1. **撓, 搔, 抓, 摩擦, 搓** — scrape or rub as if to relieve itching
*“Don't scratch your insect bites!”*
2. **發癢, 癢** — have or perceive an itch
*“I'm itching--the air is so dry!”*
3. *(intransitive)* **渴望** — To have a constant, teasing urge; to feel strongly motivated; to want or desire something.
4. *(intransitive, stative)* **癢 /癢** — To feel itchy; to feel a need to be scratched.
5. cause to perceive an itch
*“his skin itched”*
6. have a strong desire or urge to do something
*“She is itching to start the project”*
*“He is spoiling for a fight”*
*n.* 1. **渴望** — A constant teasing desire or want.
2. **衝動, 渴望** — a strong restless desire
*“why this urge to travel?”*
3. **癢 /癢, 發癢 /發癢** — A sensation felt on an area of the skin that causes a person or animal to want to scratch said area.
4. **疥瘡, 疥癬, 疥癬病** — a contagious skin infection caused by the itch mite; characterized by persistent itching and skin irritation
*“he has a bad case of the itch”*
5. an irritating cutaneous sensation that produces a desire to scratch

## item

**發音：** /ˈaɪ.təm/
*n.* 1. **細節, 詳情** — a small part that can be considered separately from the whole
*“it was perfect in all details”*
2. **細目, 條款** — a whole individual unit; especially when included in a list or collection
*“they reduced the price on many items”*
3. **細節** — an isolated fact that is considered separately from the whole
*“several of the details are similar”*
*“a point of information”*
4. **項目 /項目, 東西 /東西** — A distinct physical object.
5. *(broadly)* **寶物 /寶物, 物品** — An object that can be picked up for later use.
6. **標誌** — an individual instance of a type of symbol
*“the word`error' contains three tokens of `r'”*
*adv.* 1. (used when listing or enumerating items) also
*“a length of chain, item a hook"-Philip Guedalla”*

<a id="entry-i-002841"></a>
## itemize

**發音：** /ˈaɪtəmaɪz/
*v.* 1. *(ambitransitive)* **詳細列舉 /詳細列舉, 分條列述 /分條列述** — To state in items, or by particulars
2. specify individually
*“She enumerated the many obstacles she had encountered”*
*“The doctor recited the list of possible side effects of the drug”*
3. place on a list of items
*“itemize one's tax deductions”*

3. **循環** — (computer science) a single execution of a set of instructions that are to be repeated
*“the solution took hundreds of iterations”*
4. doing or saying again; a repeated performance
